Conclusion: AML使用通用代数归纳偏置，无需任务特定偏置（如图像的CNN或表格的XGBoost），且无需交叉验证和超参数调优，在两种不同类型的数据集上均取得了有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Symbolic methods are generally not considered competitive with strong modern learners on realistic supervised tasks. We evaluate Algebraic Machine Learning (AML), a framework that learns through subdirect decomposition of algebraic structure rather than numerical optimization, against standard baselines on image and tabular classification across varying training-set sizes. We find that AML trained only on training data without using validation or cross-validation outperforms a family of cross-validated baseline methods including CNNs on small to medium image datasets (50--2000 training examples). On tabular datasets in the same size range, XGBoost is overall the best performing method, but AML is nonetheless comparable to methods incorporating task-specific biases such as LightGBM and random forests. AML achieves this competitive performance across two very different types of datasets using a generic algebraic inductive bias, rather than the modality-specific biases built into standard baselines like CNNs for images or XGBoost for tabular data, and requires no cross validation because it has no task-dependent hyperparameters to tune.

</details>


### [180] [One-Way Policy Optimization for Self-Evolving LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22156)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Kexin Huang,Chiyu Ma,Shaohang Wei,Yuyang Liu,Guoyin Wang,Jingren Zhou,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLVR中二元验证器奖励稀疏导致的训练不稳定问题，本文提出OWPO方法，通过解耦优化方向与更新幅度，对劣偏差加速对齐、对优偏差增益锁定，并引入迭代参考更新形成棘轮效应，实现无需外部参考模型的持续自进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中二元验证器奖励稀疏导致训练效率低和优化不稳定，现有方法通过相对参考策略的token级约束来稳定训练，但这类约束无差别地惩罚所有偏差，当策略试图超越参考时可能翻转验证器确定的优化方向，从而抑制性能提升。

Method: 提出One-Way Policy Optimization (OWPO)，核心思想是解耦优化方向与更新幅度：验证器决定更新方向，参考策略仅用于调整幅度。具体包含非对称重加权——劣偏差（策略落后于参考）采用加速对齐，优偏差（策略超越参考）采用增益锁定；同时引入迭代参考更新，形成持续巩固增益的棘轮效应。

Result: 实验结果表明OWPO在多个强基线（DAPO、OPD、MOPD）上取得更优性能，突破了固定先验的瓶颈，实现了无需外部参考模型的持续自我进化。

Conclusion: OWPO通过解耦优化方向与幅度、非对称重加权及迭代参考更新，有效解决了RLVR中奖励稀疏和参考策略抑制问题，为LLM推理能力的持续自进化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a promising paradigm for scaling reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the sparsity of binary verifier rewards often leads to low efficiency and optimization instability. To stabilize training, existing methods typically impose token-level constraints relative to a reference policy. We identify that such constraints penalize deviations indiscriminately; this can flip verifier-determined direction when the policy attempts to outperform the reference, thereby suppressing gains. To resolve this, we propose One-Way Policy Optimization (OWPO), a method based on the principle of decoupling optimization direction from update magnitude. In OWPO, the verifier dictates the update direction, while the reference policy serves only to adjust the magnitude. Specifically, OWPO applies asymmetric reweighting: it performs Accelerated Alignment for inferior deviations (where the policy lags behind the reference) and Gain Locking for superior deviations (where the policy surpasses the reference). Furthermore, by incorporating iterative reference updates, OWPO creates a ``Ratchet Effect'' that continuously consolidates gains. Experimental results demonstrate that OWPO outperforms strong baselines, including DAPO, OPD, and MOPD, breaking the bottleneck of fixed priors to enable continuous self-evolution without reliance on external reference models.

</details>


### [181] [Maestro: Reinforcement Learning to Orchestrate Hierarchical Model-Skill Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22177)
*Jinyang Wu,Guocheng Zhai,Ruihan Jin,Yuhao Shen,Zhengxi Lu,Fan Zhang,Haoran Luo,Zheng Lian,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Maestro框架，利用强化学习驱动的策略，在层次化模型-技能注册表上动态编排冻结的专家模型和技能库，将异构多模态任务建模为序列决策过程。仅用4B大小的编排器，在十个多模态基准上平均准确率70.1%，超越GPT-5（69.3%）和Gemini-2.5-Pro（68.7%），且学到的协调策略能零样本泛化到未见过的模型和技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体框架通常依赖单一的大语言模型和固定逻辑来调用技能，无法利用不同模型在不同领域的互补优势，限制了在下游任务上的性能表现。

Method: 将异构多模态任务重构为层次化模型-技能注册表上的序贯决策过程；训练轻量级策略（4B编排器），通过基于结果的强化学习优化，无需步骤级监督；策略在每个步骤决定是否调用外部专家、选择哪个模型-技能对以及何时终止。

Result: 在十个代表性多模态基准上平均准确率70.1%，超越GPT-5（69.3%）和Gemini-2.5-Pro（68.7%）；协调策略可泛化到未见过的模型和技能，在四个挑战性基准上平均59.5%，超越所有闭源基线；同时保持高计算效率和低延迟。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习驱动的轻量级编排策略，Maestro有效地组合了多个冻结专家模型的互补优势，无需重新训练即可适应新模型和技能，为构建更灵活、高效的多模态智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: The proliferation of large language models (LLMs) and modular skills has endowed autonomous agents with increasingly powerful capabilities. Existing frameworks typically rely on monolithic LLMs and fixed logic to interface with these skills. This gives rise to a critical bottleneck: different LLMs offer distinct advantages across diverse domains, yet current frameworks fail to exploit the complementary strengths of models and skills, thereby limiting their performance on downstream tasks. In this paper, we present Maestro (Multimodal Agent for Expert-Skill Targeted Reinforced Orchestration), a Reinforcement Learning (RL)-driven orchestration framework that reframes heterogeneous multimodal tasks as a sequential decision-making process over a hierarchical model-skill registry. Rather than consolidating all knowledge into a single model, Maestro trains a lightweight policy to dynamically compose ensembles of frozen expert models and a two-tier skill library, deciding at each step whether to invoke an external expert, which model-skill pair to select, and when to terminate. The policy is optimized via outcome-based RL, requiring no step-level supervision. We evaluate Maestro across ten representative multimodal benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, chart understanding, high-resolution perception, and domain-specific analysis. With only a 4B orchestrator, Maestro achieves an average accuracy of 70.1%, surpassing both GPT-5 (69.3%) and Gemini-2.5-Pro (68.7%). Crucially, the learned coordination policy generalizes to unseen models and skills without retraining: augmenting the registry with out-of-domain experts yields a 59.5% average on four challenging benchmarks, outperforming all closed-source baselines. Maestro further maintains high computational efficiency with low latency. The source code is available at https://github.com/jinyangwu/Maestro.

</details>


### [182] [Survive or Collapse: The Asymmetric Roles of Data Gating and Reward Grounding in Self-Play RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22217)
*Sophia Xiao Pu,Zhaotian Weng,Chengzhi Liu,Jayanth Srinivasa,Gaowen Liu,William Yang Wang,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验发现，自对弈强化学习的稳定性主要由数据级别的严格门控（gate）决定，而非奖励信号设计。门控机制足以在所有测试的奖励变体下保证稳定性，而移除门控后任何奖励设计都无法维持稳定。研究还揭示了“有根据的提议者悖论”：拥有真实标签的提议者与自一致性求解器配对时，反而会加速模型崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自对弈强化学习在提升推理能力方面取得了显著成果，但普遍存在训练崩溃和不稳定的问题，且现有研究主要将其归因于奖励设计问题，缺乏对稳定性根本机制的深入理解。

Method: 在Python输出预测任务和确定性DSL孪生任务上设计控制实验，系统比较了数据门控（gate）和奖励信号两个变量对自对弈稳定性的影响，测试了多种奖励变体（包括无真实标签的自一致性奖励），并引入连续严格参数ε分析相变特征。

Result: 严格的门控机制在所有奖励变体下均能保证稳定性；移除门控后任何奖励设计都无法阻止崩溃。拥有真实标签的提议者与自一致性求解器配对时崩溃更快。连续门控参数ε显示训练指标在低ε处解耦，而验证准确率在更高ε处才下降，呈现两阶段相变。

Conclusion: 数据级别的门控是自对弈稳定性的约束瓶颈，而非奖励校准；稳定性由两个独立杠杆不对称地控制，其中数据门控起决定性作用。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning trains language models on their own generated tasks, co-evolving a proposer and solver without human labels. Recent systems report strong reasoning gains, but collapse and instability are widely observed and poorly understood. The dominant response treats this as a reward-design problem. We argue instead that self-play stability is governed by two distinct levers: a data-level gate that decides which proposer-generated tasks enter the training pool, and the reward signal that updates the policy on tasks already admitted. Through controlled experiments on a Python output-prediction task and a deterministic-DSL twin task that strips pretraining priors, output ambiguity, and executor noise, we find the two levers are asymmetric. A strict gate is sufficient for stability under every reward variant we test, including a self-consistency reward with no access to ground truth; while no reward variant is sufficient once the gate is removed. This asymmetry exposes a counter-intuitive coupling we call the Grounded Proposer Paradox: a proposer with ground-truth access accelerates collapse faster than an ungrounded one when paired with a self-consistency solver, by concentrating training on clean tasks that form the fastest path to a spurious self-consistent attractor. Replacing the binary gate with a continuous strictness parameter $\varepsilon$ further reveals a two-stage phase transition: training-side metrics decouple at low $\varepsilon$, while validation accuracy holds until $\varepsilon$ is much higher. Data-level gating, not reward calibration, is the binding constraint on self-play stability.

</details>


### [183] [Boundary-targeted Membership Inference Attacks on Safety Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22373)
*Anthony Hughes,Alexander Goldberg,Prince Jha,Adam Perer,Nikolaos Aletras,Niloofar Mireshghallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对安全分类器的边界目标选择策略，通过识别分类器最不自信的训练样本来增强成员推理攻击（MIA）的效果。实验表明，该方法能以5%假阳性率恢复19%的标记为情绪困扰的对话，比现有最先进MIA方法高出3.5倍，并发现基于内容的过滤无效，而噪声策略可有效缓解此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分类器在训练时使用了包含自残和心理健康讨论的敏感数据集，存在隐私泄露风险。然而，针对这些模型的成员推理攻击（MIA）尚未被充分研究。本文旨在探究如何利用分类器对某些样本的低置信度这一局部泛化失败现象，来更有效地推断训练集成员身份。

Method: 提出一种边界目标选择策略（boundary-targeted selection strategy），识别分类器对其最不自信的样本（即位于决策边界附近的低置信度样本），这些样本因依赖记忆而非泛化来解决歧义，从而放大了成员身份的信号。该方法作为MIA的预处理步骤，先筛选出易受攻击的样本，再执行标准MIA。

Result: 在微调用于检测需要情感支持的用户的分类器上，攻击者能以5%的假阳性率恢复19%的被标记为用户情绪困扰的对话，比单独使用最先进的MIA方法高出3.5倍。基于内容的过滤无法有效保护这类样本，而现有的噪声策略可以有效缓解模型对此类边界的易感性。

Conclusion: 安全分类器中存在边界样本容易遭受成员推理攻击，且传统内容过滤无效；但噪声策略可提供有效防御。本研究揭示了安全分类器训练数据隐私保护的新挑战与应对方向。

Abstract: Safety classifiers are essential safeguards within generative AI systems, filtering harmful content or identifying at-risk users when interacting with large language models. Despite their necessity, these models are trained on sensitive datasets including discussions of self-harm and mental health, raising important, yet poorly understood, privacy concerns. Membership inference attacks (MIAs) allow adversaries to infer membership of examples used to train models. In this work, we hypothesize that identifying the examples on which the classifier is least confident are informative for an adversary to infer membership. This reflects a localized failure of generalization, where the model relies on memorization to resolve ambiguity in the training set. To investigate this, we introduce a new boundary-targeted selection strategy that identifies low confidence examples that amplify the signal of an examples membership within a training set. Our experimental results show that an adversary can recover 19\% of the conversations a safety classifier flagged as indicating user distress, at a 5\% false-positive rate, on a classifier fine-tuned for detecting a user who may require emotional support. This is $3.5$ times more than attacking using state-of-the-art MIA methods alone. Finally, we characterize the boundary laying examples and show that content-based filtering is ineffective for protection, and existing noise strategies can effectively mitigate susceptibility of these examples.

</details>


### [184] [IKNO: Infinite-order Kernel Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22182)
*Pengyuan Zhu,Ivor W. Tsang,Yueming Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无限阶核神经算子（IKNO），通过无限阶核积分构造神经算子，并给出优雅的闭合有限近似形式。IKNO包括两种变体：IKNO-Vanilla（全核预解式）和IKNO-TP（张量积预解式），在保持高效计算的同时实现全局信息聚合，在多种基准数据集上达到SOTA精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子主要依赖一阶核积分近似，表达能力严重受限，无法充分刻画复杂物理系统的全局依赖关系。

Method: 提出无限阶核神经算子（IKNO），通过无限阶核积分构造算子，推导出闭合有限近似形式。设计两种变体：IKNO-Vanilla基于Kronecker特征分解在全乘积网格上应用全核预解式；IKNO-TP通过逐轴预解式组合构建张量积算子。同时开发了两种变体的快速计算方案。

Result: 在时间依赖和时间无关的多种基准数据集（包括大规模工业数据集）上，IKNO方法在几乎所有基准上一致达到SOTA精度，且能扩展到超大规模点云数据。

Conclusion: 无限阶核积分构造能有效突破一阶近似的表达瓶颈，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升神经算子的全局信息聚合能力与预测精度。

Abstract: Neural operators have achieved significant success in modern scientific computing due to their flexibility and strong generalization capabilities. Existing models, however, primarily rely on first-order kernel integral approximations, which severely limit their expressivity. To address this, we propose the Infinite-order Kernel Neural Operator (IKNO), which constructs neural operators via infinite-order kernel integrals and admits an elegant closed-form finite approximation. We develop two complementary infinite-order neural operator constructions: IKNO-Vanilla, which applies the full-kernel resolvent on the product grid via Kronecker eigendecomposition, and IKNO-TP, an alternative tensor-product operator that composes per-axis resolvents. Furthermore, we develop fast computation schemes for both variants of IKNO, which achieve outstanding global information aggregation while maintaining high computational efficiency. Empirically, we evaluate our IKNO on both time-dependent and time-independent benchmarks with arbitrary input shapes, including large-scale industrial datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the IKNO method consistently achieves the SOTA accuracy with significant improvements on nearly all benchmark datasets while maintaining scalability to very large point clouds.

</details>


### [185] [Structured-Sparse Attention for Entity Tracking with Subquadratic Sequence Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22476)
*Hangyue Zhao,Paul Caillon,Erwan Fagnou,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于块状分解的解析算子，用于高效评估实体跟踪中的注意力机制。通过利用注意力权重的局部块对角结构，将跨块交互通过降阶系统路由，实现了序列长度的次二次复杂度（O(n^{4/3}d)），在受控跟踪基准上匹配密集算子精度，加速12-29%，最高可比紧凑密集Transformer快2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体跟踪需要在长序列上维护和更新实体与属性的潜在状态。现有的任务特定注意力算子通过单层内的多跳状态传播压缩深层Transformer，但其密集评估仍然计算昂贵，需要更高效的推理方法。

Method: 观察到学习到的注意力具有强结构化特性：大部分质量集中在局部块对角邻域，仅有少量跨块残差。据此推导出解析算子的块状评估方法，保持块内交互精确，通过降阶系统处理跨块交互，实现了次二次复杂度O(n^{4/3}d)（当d≈n时为O(n^{7/3})）。

Result: 在受控跟踪基准上，该方法匹配密集算子的准确性，在标准化测量协议下减少12-29%的墙钟时间，在可比精确匹配准确率下比紧凑密集Transformer快2.4倍。同时发现当同时演化的属性数量超过注意力头数时性能会崩溃。

Conclusion: 利用注意力结构的局部块对角特性可以显著加速实体跟踪中的注意力计算，但该方法存在容量限制，需注意注意力头数与跟踪属性数量的匹配关系。

Abstract: Entity tracking requires maintaining and updating latent states for entities and attributes over long sequences. Recent task-specific attention operators can compress deep Transformer stacks into a few layers by performing multi-hop state propagation within a single layer, but their dense evaluation remains expensive. We show that in this setting, learned attention is strongly structured: most mass concentrates in local block-diagonal neighborhoods with a light cross-block residue. Exploiting this, we derive a blockwise evaluation of a resolvent-style operator that keeps within-block interactions exact and routes cross-block interactions through a reduced system. The resulting evaluation is subquadratic in sequence length $O(n^{4/3}d)$ (and $O(n^{7/3})$ when $d\approx n$). On controlled tracking benchmarks, our method matches the dense operator's accuracy while reducing wall-clock time by $12-29\%$ under a standardized measurement protocol, and is up to $2.4 \times$ faster than a compact dense Transformer at comparable exact-match accuracy. We further provide ablations over block size and model capacity, and identify a limitation: performance collapses when the number of simultaneously evolving properties exceeds the number of attention heads.

</details>


### [186] [Bandit Convex Optimization with Gradient Prediction Adaptivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22191)
*Shuche Wang,Adarsh Barik,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带梯度预测的凸赌博机在线学习问题。在单点反馈下证明了即使预测误差很小时仍存在Ω(√T)的遗憾下界；在两点反馈下提出了TP-VR-OPT算法，通过方差缩减的梯度估计器实现了O(√(d·E[S_T]))的预测自适应遗憾界，并证明了该界在维度因子上是最优的。进一步扩展到自适应版本和非平稳环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究乐观梯度预测能否以预测自适应的方式改善凸赌博机在线学习在对抗性环境下的最坏情况遗憾保证，即遗憾界能否随累积预测误差S_T的减小而降低。

Method: 提出Two-Point Variance-Reduced Optimistic Gradient Descent (TP-VR-OPT)算法，核心是设计了一个方差与预测误差而非梯度范数成比例的方差缩减梯度估计器，用于两点反馈设置。

Result: 1) 单点反馈下存在Ω(√T)的不可避免下界，即使S_T=o(T)；2) 两点反馈下TP-VR-OPT达到O(√(d·E[S_T]))的遗憾界；3) 信息论下界为Ω(√(E[S_T]))，证明了TP-VR-OPT在维度因子√d内最优；4) 开发了无需先验知识的自适应变体；5) 扩展到非平稳环境获得动态遗憾保证。

Conclusion: 在两点反馈设置下，梯度预测可以有效改善凸赌博机学习的遗憾界，且TP-VR-OPT算法在理论上近乎最优；但在单点反馈下，梯度估计的方差从根本上掩盖了预测的益处。

Abstract: Bandit convex optimization (BCO) is a fundamental online learning framework with partial feedback, where the learner observes only the loss incurred at the chosen decision point in each round. In this work, we investigate whether optimistic gradient predictions can improve worst-case regret guarantees in a prediction-adaptive manner. Specifically, given gradient predictions $m_t$, we seek regret bounds that scale with the cumulative prediction error $S_T=\sum_{t=1}^T \|\nabla f_t(x_t)-m_t\|^2.$ We first establish a negative result: under the single-point feedback protocol, an unavoidable $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ regret lower bound persists even when $S_T=o(T)$, showing that the variance of gradient estimation fundamentally obscures the benefit of accurate predictions. To overcome this barrier, we propose \emph{Two-Point Variance-Reduced Optimistic Gradient Descent} (TP-VR-OPT) for the two-point feedback setting. The key idea is a novel variance-reduced gradient estimator whose variance scales with the prediction error rather than the gradient norm. This yields a regret bound of $O\big(\sqrt{d\,\mathbb{E}[S_T]}\big),$ where $d$ is the decision dimension. Complementing this result, we establish an information-theoretic lower bound that scales as $Ω(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[S_T]})$, providing a fundamental characterization of the best achievable prediction-adaptive regret and showing that TP-VR-OPT is optimal up to a factor of $\sqrt d$. We further develop adaptive variants that eliminate the need for prior knowledge of $\mathbb{E}[S_T]$ or the horizon $T$, and extend our framework to non-stationary environments, establishing dynamic regret guarantees that adapt simultaneously to the cumulative prediction error and the comparator path length.

</details>


### [187] [Reinforced Graph of Thoughts: RL-Driven Adaptive Prompting for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22195)
*Manuel Noah Riesen,Peter Alfred von Niederhäusern*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出强化思维图（RGoT），利用强化学习自动生成思维图的操作图，替代传统手动定义方式，实现自适应任务复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GoT框架中操作图需要人工手动定义，缺乏适应性；现有方法静态刚性，无法根据问题复杂性动态调整。

Method: 基于强化学习（RL）从人工定义的操作集合中自适应生成操作图，替代原始GoT中的静态手动定义。

Result: 在一定约束条件下，能够以自动化方式构建适应任务复杂度的操作图。

Conclusion: RGoT实现了GoT提示范式的自动化，通过RL实现了操作图的自适应生成，提升了框架的灵活性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Graph of Thoughts (GoT), a generalized form of recent prompting paradigms for large language models (LLMs), has been shown to be useful for elaborate problem solving. By executing a graph of operations, thoughts of the LLM are structured as an arbitrary graph, forming the actual graph of thoughts. Originally, the graph of operations is defined manually, which requires in-depth knowledge about the solution of the problem to solve. Such a static graph of operations is rigid and therefore lacks adaptability. We propose Reinforced Graph of Thoughts (RGoT), an automated approach to the GoT prompting paradigm that leverages reinforcement learning (RL) to adaptively generate a graph of operations from a human-defined set. Results indicate that, under certain constraints, it is possible to construct graphs of operations adaptively to the task's complexity in an automated way.

</details>


### [188] [A Tutorial on Diffusion Theory: From Differential Equations to Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22586)
*Jiayi Fu,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该教程从微分方程视角系统阐述了扩散模型，涵盖条件高斯前向过程的ODE/SDE表示、边际化前向动力学、反向SDE/概率流ODE、分数匹配训练目标、DPM-Solver采样方法、分类器指导和无分类器指导，以及DDPM/DDIM与反向SDE/ODE框架的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从微分方程的统一视角重新审视扩散模型，为理解和推导扩散模型的前向与反向动力学提供严谨的数学基础，并厘清不同采样方法（DDPM、DDIM、DPM-Solver）之间的内在联系。

Method: 首先建立条件高斯前向过程的ODE和SDE表示，通过边际化得到数据分布到高斯先验的传输方程；然后推导反向SDE和概率流ODE，以边际分数为核心；训练目标采用分数匹配（等价于噪声预测），采样方法涵盖DPM-Solver、分类器指导和无分类器指导；最后将DDPM和DDIM分别对应为反向SDE和反向ODE的离散化采样。

Result: 明确了扩散模型前向与反向动力学的微分方程框架，证明了噪声预测目标与分数匹配的等价性，揭示了DDPM与DDIM在训练目标一致但采样路径不同（SDE vs ODE）的本质区别。

Conclusion: 微分方程视角为扩散模型提供了统一且严谨的理论框架，有助于理解其训练与采样机制，并为后续算法改进（如高效求解器、可控生成）奠定了数学基础。

Abstract: This tutorial develops diffusion models from the viewpoint of differential equations. We begin with the conditional Gaussian forward process and show that this path admits both an ordinary differential equation (ODE) representation and a stochastic differential equation (SDE) representation. Averaging the conditional process over the data distribution then yields marginalized forward ODE and SDE formulations that transport the data distribution $p_0=p_{\mathrm{data}}$ to a Gaussian prior $p_1=\mathcal{N}(0,I)$. We next derive the corresponding reverse-time dynamics, namely the reverse SDE and the reverse probability-flow ODE, both of which are governed by the marginal score $\grad\log p_t(x)$. This leads to a training objective for score estimation and shows that the standard noise-prediction objective is equivalent to score matching up to an additive constant independent of the model parameters. We then discuss sampling methods for the learned reverse dynamics, including DPM-Solver, as well as guided sampling through classifier guidance and classifier-free guidance. Finally, we compare DDPM and DDIM with the reverse SDE/ODE framework and show that they share the same training objective, while DDPM sampling corresponds to discrete reverse-SDE sampling and DDIM sampling corresponds to reverse-ODE sampling.

</details>


### [189] [Two is better than one: A Collapse-free Multi-Reward RLIF Training Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22620)
*Shourov Joarder,Diganta Sikdar,Ahsan Habib Akash,Binod Bhattarai,Prashnna Gyawali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多奖励RLIF框架，通过聚类投票的答案级奖励和token级自置信度的完成级奖励，结合GDPO归一化和KL-Cov正则化，在不依赖外部监督的情况下提升LLM推理稳定性，性能接近有监督RLVR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLIF方法依赖单一内部奖励信号，容易导致奖励破解、熵坍塌和推理结构退化问题。

Method: 构建互补的双奖励信号：答案级奖励（基于聚类投票）和完成级奖励（基于token级自置信度）；采用GDPO归一化缓解奖励尺度不平衡；引入KL-Cov正则化针对低熵token分布进行惩罚，防止熵坍塌。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，该方法优于现有无监督RL方法，性能接近有监督RLVR方法。

Conclusion: 互补的内部奖励结合定向正则化，可以在不依赖外部真实标注的情况下支持稳定的长程推理。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved the reasoning ability of LLMs, but often depends on external supervision from human annotations or gold-standard solutions. Reinforcement learning from internal feedback (RLIF) has recently emerged as a scalable unsupervised alternative, using signals extracted from the model itself. However, existing RLIF methods typically rely on a single internal reward, which can lead to reward hacking, entropy collapse, and degraded reasoning structure. We propose a multi-reward RLIF framework that decomposes the training signal into two complementary components: an answer-level reward based on cluster voting and a completion-level reward based on token-wise self-certainty. To combine these signals robustly, we apply GDPO-based normalization to reduce reward-scale imbalance. We further introduce KL-Cov regularization, which targets low-entropy token distributions responsible for disproportionate entropy reduction, preserving exploration and preventing late-stage collapse. Across mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, our method improves stability and robustness over prior unsupervised RL approaches, while achieving performance close to supervised RLVR methods. These results show that complementary internal rewards, combined with targeted regularization, can support stable long-horizon reasoning without relying on external ground-truth supervision. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [190] [Can Transformers Learn to Verify During Backtracking Search?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22221)
*Yin Jun Phua,Tony Ribeiro,Tuan Nguyen,Katsumi Inoue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，在累计搜索轨迹上训练的 decoder-only Transformer 模型存在两个根本问题：分散检索（状态特征散落在多个位置）和历史纠缠（预测器依赖于轨迹历史而非当前状态）。作者提出了局部化（解决分散检索）和选择性状态注意力（SSA，解决历史纠缠）两种结构性修复方法，并在 3-SAT、图着色、积木世界等任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于 Transformer 的推理系统通常在离线求解器轨迹上训练自回归 next-token 损失，但模型的输入是累积的完整搜索历史（包括回溯）。然而，最优的继续/回溯决策应仅依赖于当前搜索状态，而非到达该状态的轨迹历史。现有方法忽略了这一状态马尔可夫性要求，导致模型学习了错误的决策边界。

Method: 1) 局部化（Localization）：在轨迹层面重写每个决策块，将状态特征暴露在局部位置，解决分散检索问题。2) 选择性状态注意力（SSA）：一种固定的注意力掩码，在不修改训练数据、目标函数或参数的情况下，从结构上强制模型仅基于当前状态进行决策。实验在4个任务（3-SAT、图着色、积木世界、回溯解析）上进行，重点关注反应式验证（传播暴露矛盾后的场景）。

Result: 在状态相同但历史不同的样本对上，SSA 能生成一致的决策，而基于因果注意力的基线模型则不能。SSA 在所有四个任务上均有效解决了历史纠缠问题。

Conclusion: 作者诊断了 Transformer 在序列化轨迹数据上的行为故障，并提供了结构性修复方法。预训练语言模型在自身的推理步骤上进行搜索时可能面临同样的问题。此外，推理时上下文清理（context clearing）可以作为无需重新训练即可实现同样隔离效果的一种候选方法。

Abstract: Backtracking search underlies classical constraint solvers, planners, and theorem provers. Recent transformer-based reasoning systems explore search trees over their own intermediate steps. A common training recipe fits an autoregressive next-token loss on offline solver traces. The model's input at each step is a cumulative trace of all prior decisions. The optimal continue-or-backtrack predictor depends only on the current search state, since two trajectories reaching the same state admit the same viable continuations. We show that decoder-only transformers trained on cumulative traces fail this requirement in two ways: the trace can scatter state features across many positions (scattered retrieval), and the predictor can condition on the trajectory rather than the state (history entanglement). We address scattered retrieval with localization, a trace-level fix that rewrites each decision block to expose state features locally. We address history entanglement with Selective State Attention (SSA), a fixed attention mask that enforces state-based decisions structurally without modifying training data, objective, or parameters. We focus on reactive verification, after propagation has exposed a contradiction. We test SSA on 3-SAT, graph coloring, Blocks World, and backtracking parsing. On same-state pairs that differ only in prior history, SSA emits identical decisions while a cumulative-trained causal baseline does not. Our contribution is a diagnostic of transformer behavior on serialized trajectory data, paired with a structural fix. Pretrained language models that search over their own reasoning steps may face the same failure. Our analysis opens up inference-time context clearing as a candidate way to apply the same isolation without retraining.

</details>


### [191] [How Many Different Outputs Can a Transformer Generate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22223)
*Maxime Meyer,Mario Michelessa,Caroline Chaux,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅利用Transformer架构的少数特征，即可在定性和定量上准确预测其能够输出的不同序列数量。作者给出了基于提示长度的上界，并在多种架构和模型规模上验证了该上界与实际值相差不超过一个数量级。研究还从理论上解释了先前实验中Transformer在简单序列任务（如复制和填塞）上失败的原因。形式上证明了：(i)可访问序列（即Transformer能针对某提示输出的序列）的最大长度随提示长度线性增长；(ii)超过临界阈值后，可访问序列的比例随序列长度指数衰减；(iii)提示长度与可访问序列长度之间的线性系数存在理论上限。值得注意的是，这些结论即使在无限上下文和无限计算时间的条件下也成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer架构的输出能力边界，解释其在简单序列任务上失败的原因，并为预测Transformer能生成的不同序列数量提供理论依据。

Method: 利用Transformer架构的少量关键特征，从理论上推导序列可访问性的上界，并通过实验在多种架构和模型规模上进行验证。

Result: 提示长度与可访问序列最大长度呈线性关系，超过临界阈值后可访问序列比例指数衰减，且线性系数存在理论上界；理论预测与实验结果相差不超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: Transformer的输出能力受限于其架构固有特性，即使在无限上下文和计算时间下也存在根本性的序列长度瓶颈，这解释了其在某些简单序列任务上的失败。

Abstract: We study how we can leverage only a handful of characteristics of a transformer's architecture to closely predict the number of different sequences it can output, both qualitatively and quantitatively. We provide an upper bound depending on the length of the prompt, which we show empirically to be tight up to a factor less than 10, across architectures and model sizes. Our analysis also provides a theoretical explanation for previously observed empirical failures of transformers on simple sequence tasks, such as copying and cramming. Formally, we prove that (i) the maximal length of accessible sequences (those that the transformer can output for some prompt) grows linearly with the prompt length, (ii) beyond a critical threshold, the proportion of accessible sequences decays exponentially with sequence length, and (iii) the linear coefficient relating prompt length to accessible sequence length admits a theoretical upper bound. Notably, these results hold even with unbounded context and computation time.

</details>


### [192] [Vector Policy Optimization: Training for Diversity Improves Test-Time Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22817)
*Ryan Bahlous-Boldi,Isha Puri,Idan Shenfeld,Akarsh Kumar,Mehul Damani,Sebastian Risi,Omar Khattab,Zhang-Wei Hong,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VPO是一种替代GRPO的RL算法，通过向量奖励训练LLM生成多样化解决方案，在推理时搜索中超越标量RL基线，尤其在进化搜索中解决GRPO无法解决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练范式优化预定义标量奖励，导致输出低熵响应分布，缺乏推理时搜索所需的多样性。而实际中奖励往往是向量形式的（如代码生成的逐测试用例正确性、多种用户画像或奖励模型），需要让LLM适应多样化的下游奖励函数并产生多样化解决方案。

Method: 提出Vector Policy Optimization (VPO)，一种RL算法，作为GRPO优势估计器的即插即用替代方案。VPO利用实践中奖励的向量值特性，训练LLM输出一组解决方案，使个体解决方案在向量奖励空间中专门针对不同权衡进行特化。

Result: 在四个任务上，VPO在测试时搜索（如pass@k和best@k）中匹敌或超越最强标量RL基线，且随着搜索预算增加差距扩大。在进化搜索中，VPO模型解决了GRPO模型完全无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 随着测试时搜索变得越来越标准化，优化多样性可能需成为默认的后训练目标。

Abstract: Language models must now generalize out of the box to novel environments and work inside inference-scaling search procedures, such as AlphaEvolve, that select rollouts with a variety of task-specific reward functions. Unfortunately, the standard paradigm of LLM post-training optimizes a pre-specified scalar reward, often leading current LLMs to produce low-entropy response distributions and thus to struggle at displaying the diversity that inference-time search will require. We propose Vector Policy Optimization (VPO), an RL algorithm that explicitly trains policies to anticipate diverse downstream reward functions and to produce diverse solutions. VPO exploits that rewards are often vector-valued in practice, like per-test-case correctness in code generation or, say, multiple different user personas or reward models. VPO is essentially a drop-in replacement for the GRPO advantage estimator, but it trains the LLM to output a set of solutions where individual solutions specialize to different trade-offs in the vector reward space. Across four tasks, VPO matches or beats the strongest scalar RL baselines on test-time search (e.g. pass@k and best@k), with the gap widening as the search budget grows. For evolutionary search, VPO models unlock problems that GRPO models cannot solve at all. As test-time search becomes more standardized, optimizing for diversity may need to become the default post-training objective.

</details>


### [193] [Decomposing Ensemble Spread in Lorenz '96 With Learned Stochastic Parameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22242)
*Birgit Kühbacher,Daan Crommelin,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用两尺度Lorenz 1996系统作为受控测试平台，系统性地研究了混沌天气和气候预报中不同不确定性来源（固有变率、初始条件扰动、随机模型不确定性）的相互作用，发现集合扰动不增加长期方差，而是调节轨迹去相关速度；具有时间持久结构的随机参数化方案能有效提升早期集合扩散和预报一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气预报和气候预测由于混沌动力学、初始条件不完善以及物理过程表示不完整而存在固有不确定性。现有的集合预报方法往往存在欠离散问题（集合离散度增长过慢，跟不上预报误差增长），需要系统地厘清不同不确定性来源如何在混沌系统中相互作用，以指导随机参数化方案的设计与评估。

Method: 以两尺度Lorenz 1996系统作为可控测试平台，系统设计多种集合配置和参数化策略进行对比，包括现有的确定性方法、自回归方法以及新颖的贝叶斯方法和基于流的方法，通过比较不同配置下的集合离散度、轨迹去相关速度和长期方差等指标进行分析。

Result: 集合扰动不增加系统的长期方差，而是调节轨迹去相关和探索不变测度的速度；随机参数化方案，尤其是具有时间持久结构的方案，能够增强早期集合离散度增长，改善离散度-误差一致性。

Conclusion: 厘清了混沌系统中不同不确定性来源的相互作用机制，为天气和气候模型中随机参数化方案的设计与评估提供了指导性建议。

Abstract: Weather and climate forecasts are inherently uncertain due to chaotic dynamics, imperfect initial conditions, and incomplete representation of the underlying physical processes. Operational ensemble forecasts aim to represent these uncertainties through forecast spread, yet many approaches yield underdispersive estimates, with spread that grows too slowly relative to forecast error. Using the two-scale Lorenz 1996 system as a widely used, controlled testbed, we design a systematic approach to disentangle intrinsic variability, initial-condition perturbations, and stochastic model uncertainty. We compare multiple ensemble configurations and parameterization strategies, including existing deterministic and autoregressive as well as novel Bayesian and flow-based approaches. Our results show that ensemble perturbations do not increase the system's long-term variance; rather, they regulate how rapidly trajectories decorrelate and explore the invariant measure. Stochastic parameterizations, particularly those with temporally persistent structure, enhance early spread growth and improve spread-error consistency. Overall, we bring clarity to how different sources of uncertainty interact in a chaotic system and provide guidance for the design and evaluation of stochastic parameterizations in weather and climate models.

</details>


### [194] [Explainable AI for Data-Driven Design of High-Dimensional Predictive Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22243)
*Junyu Yan,Damian Machlanski,Kurt Butler,Panagiotis Dimitrakopoulos,Ewen M Harrison,Bruce Guthrie,Sotirios A Tsaftaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种探索性AI推荐框架，利用可解释AI技术为传统可解释统计模型（如Cox比例风险模型）提供三类数据驱动建议（特征排除、非线性项、特征交互），在跌倒风险预测任务中将C-index从0.805提升至0.815，并在两个公开数据集上验证了泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维健康数据的手工预测建模耗时且难以优化，而复杂黑箱模型虽性能优异但缺乏临床可解释性和信任度。需要一种既能保持模型透明度又能提升预测性能的方法。

Method: 开发了一个探索性AI推荐框架：利用灵活AI模型捕捉复杂数据模式，结合可解释AI技术将模式转化为三类具体建议（特征排除、非线性项、特征交互），用于增强现有可解释统计模型。

Result: 在245,614名患者的跌倒风险预测中，模型推荐排除23个特征、加入2个非线性项和221个特征交互，C-index从0.805提升至0.815，校准度改善（截距从-0.006到0.003；斜率从1.063到0.950），所有推荐均有文献支持。在另外两个公开数据集上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 探索性AI推荐框架展示了可解释AI和数据驱动研究设计在提升高维透明预测模型开发过程和性能方面的潜力。

Abstract: Predictive modelling is important for health data analysis and data-driven clinical decision-making. However, predictive studies are challenging to design optimally by hand when tens or even hundreds of features require selection, transformation, or interaction modelling. While complex machine learning models offer high performance, their "black-box" nature limits the clinical trust, transparency, and interpretability required for decision-making. We developed and evaluated an Exploratory AI Recommender that provides data-driven recommendations to improve predictive performance of existing interpretable statistical models. The developed framework uses flexible AI modelling to capture complex data patterns and explainable AI techniques to translate the patterns into three recommendation types: feature exclusion, non-linear terms, and feature interactions. We evaluated the framework by comparing predictive performance of a baseline (i.e., no interactions or non-linear terms) Cox Proportional Hazards (CPH) model against an augmented CPH incorporating recommendations suggested by our method. The primary analysis predicts the time to the first occurrence of a fall or related injury in 245,614 patients. Our method recommended excluding 23 features, including non-linear terms for two features, and including 221 suggested feature interactions. The C-index improved from 0.805 (95% CI 0.798-0.812) to 0.815 (95% CI 0.809-0.822), and so did calibration (intercept: -0.006 to 0.003; slope: 1.063 to 0.950). All recommendations were supported by existing literature. The method also proved effective on two additional public datasets, demonstrating wider applicability. The proposed Exploratory AI Recommender demonstrates the potential of explainable AI and data-driven study design to improve the process of developing, and the performance of high-dimensional transparent predictive models.

</details>


### [195] [What are the Right Symmetries for Formal Theorem Proving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22257)
*Krzysztof Olejniczak,Radoslav Dimitrov,Xingyue Huang,Bernardo Cuenca Grau,Jinwoo Kim,İsmail İlkan Ceylan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型（LLM）形式定理证明器在语义等价但结构不同的表述下性能差异显著的问题，提出基于范畴论的“重写范畴”框架来形式化两种对称性（证明等变性与成功不变性），并证明状态基策略生成器天然满足证明等变性，而现有LLM证明器两者均不满足。为此提出测试时聚合等价重写的方法，理论上可在采样极限下恢复成功不变性，实验表明能提升鲁棒性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM形式定理证明器对问题表述的浅层变化高度敏感，语义等价的表述可能具有截然不同的证明成功率，这暴露了模型未能尊重形式数学中固有的结构对称性。因此需要明确“什么是形式定理证明中正确的对称性”，并探索如何使LLM证明器具备这种对称性。

Method: 1) 提出“重写范畴”（rewriting categories）的范畴论框架，捕捉证明策略（tactics）产生的组合性、通常不可逆的变换；2) 形式化两种对称性：证明等变性（proof equivariance）和成功不变性（success invariance）；3) 提出测试时聚合（test-time aggregation）方法，对输入进行多种等价重写，聚合推理结果以逼近成功不变性。

Result: 理论分析表明：状态基的下一步策略生成器天然满足证明等变性；现有LLM证明器既不满足证明等变性也不满足成功不变性。测试时聚合方法在采样极限下可恢复成功不变性，在固定推理预算下能够提升鲁棒性与性能。

Conclusion: 对称性是LLM定理证明中缺失的关键归纳偏置，测试时计算是近似实现该偏置的实用途径。

Abstract: Formal theorem provers based on large language models (LLMs) are highly sensitive to superficial variations in problem representation: semantically equivalent statements can exhibit drastically different proof success rates, revealing a failure to respect structural symmetries inherent in formal mathematics. This raises a central question: what are the right symmetries for formal theorem proving? We introduce rewriting categories, a category-theoretic framework capturing the compositional, generally non-invertible transformations induced by proof tactics, and use it to formalize two symmetry notions: proof equivariance, governing how proof distributions transform under rewrites, and success invariance (i.e., invariance of success probability), requiring equivalent statements to be solved with the same probability. We observe that state-based next-tactic provers naturally satisfy proof equivariance by operating on proof states. In contrast, state-of-the-art LLM-based provers satisfy neither property, exhibiting large performance variation across equivalent formulations. To mitigate this, we propose test-time methods that aggregate over equivalent rewritings of the input, showing theoretically that they recover success invariance in the sampling limit, and empirically, that they improve robustness and performance under fixed inference budgets. Our results highlight symmetry as a key missing inductive bias in LLM-based theorem proving and suggest test-time computation as a practical route to approximate it.

</details>


### [196] [An Evidence Hierarchy for Bayesian Object Classification via OSINT-Aided Heterogeneous Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22259)
*Jan Nausner,Michael Hubner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种上下文感知和领域知识增强的融合方法，通过建立新的证据层次结构（直接、指示性和上下文信息）、引入OSINT环境信息以及构建贝叶斯威胁分类机制（领域知识先验），在模拟场景中实现了高达95%的分类准确率，有效应对了CBRNE威胁检测中传感器异构、杂波干扰和数据稀缺等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBRNE威胁检测中异构传感器融合面临多重挑战：单个传感器只能检测部分威胁且可靠性不一，传感器端高杂波率干扰融合系统，高质量数据集稀缺限制了基于学习的检测与分类模型的发展。

Method: 1) 建立包含直接信息、指示性信息和上下文信息的新型证据层次结构；2) 通过收集、处理和利用OSINT（开源情报）输入，将环境上下文信息引入融合过程；3) 利用所有层次的证据，构建具有领域知识先验的贝叶斯威胁类型分类机制。

Result: 在模拟场景中评估，该方法在杂波鲁棒性和先验失配方面表现优异，整体分类准确率达到95%。

Conclusion: 提出的上下文感知和领域知识增强融合方法能有效缓解传感器固有缺陷，提升CBRNE威胁检测与分类的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous sensor fusion is vital for detecting, localizing, and classifying CBRNE threats. However, individual sensors are often only capable of detecting a subset of relevant threats with varying reliability or can even provide only indirect threat indications, making threat classification challenging. Furthermore, high clutter rates on the sensor side present a great challenge for fusion systems. Additionally, the limited availability of high quality datasets hinders the advancement of learning-based detection and classification models in smart sensors. To mitigate these sensor related shortcomings, a context-aware and domain knowledge-enhanced fusion process is proposed. First, a novel evidence hierarchy is established that enables modeling of direct, indicative, and contextual information. Second, contextual information about the environment is introduced into the fusion process, by collecting, processing, and exploiting OSINT inputs. Third, all levels of the evidence hierarchy are used to craft a Bayesian threat type classification mechanism with domain knowledge-informed priors. The proposed methodology is evaluated in simulated scenarios, and the results demonstrate the benefit of the proposed fusion approach in terms of robustness to clutter and prior mismatch, with an overall classification accuracy of up to 95%.

</details>


### [197] [Tailoring Teaching to Aptitude: Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22263)
*Hongbin Zhang,Chaozheng Wang,Kehai Chen,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Jinpeng Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出方向自适应自蒸馏（DASD），通过熵路由有监督方向（高熵推离教师以保持探索，低熵拉近教师以稳定执行）来改进On-policy自蒸馏在复杂推理中因抑制预测不确定性导致的性能退化问题，在六个数学推理基准上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的On-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）方法在复杂推理任务中性能退化，原因是统一方向的教师监督抑制了预测不确定性，阻碍了探索和假设修正过程。

Method: 提出方向自适应自蒸馏（DASD），将统一的教师模仿重构为熵路由有监督方向：高熵token推离特权教师以保留探索能力，低熵token拉近教师以稳定步骤级执行精度。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，DASD在宏观Avg@16指标上超过了强RLVR和自蒸馏基线。Pass@k、推理健康度和泛化分析表明性能提升来源于保留探索的同时不牺牲步骤级执行。

Conclusion: 通过熵感知的方向性监督，DASD有效解决了OPSD在复杂推理中探索与执行之间的权衡问题，验证了不同不确定性级别token需要差异化教师监督方向的观点。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is an emerging LLM post-training paradigm in which the model serves as its own teacher: conditioned on privileged information such as a reference trace or hint, the same policy provides dense token-level supervision on its own rollouts. However, recent studies show that OPSD degrades complex reasoning by suppressing predictive uncertainty, which supports exploration and hypothesis revision. Our token-level analysis shows that this failure arises from applying a uniform direction of teacher supervision across tokens with different uncertainty levels: conformity to the privileged self-teacher suppresses exploration at high entropy, while deviation from the teacher degrades step accuracy at low entropy. Accordingly, we propose \textbf{Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation} (\textbf{DASD}), which reframes privileged self-distillation from uniform teacher imitation into entropy-routed directional supervision: high-entropy tokens are pushed away from the privileged teacher to preserve exploration, while low-entropy tokens are pulled toward the teacher to stabilize step-level execution. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, DASD achieves the best macro Avg@16 over strong RLVR and self-distillation baselines. Pass@$k$, reasoning-health, and generalization analyses show that these average gains come from preserving exploration without sacrificing step-level execution.

</details>


### [198] [Detecting Atypical Clients in Federated Learning via Representation-Level Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22266)
*Cristian Pérez-Corral,Jose I. Mestre,Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Manuel F. Dolz,Enrique S. Quitana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级几何信号，通过测量客户端本地训练对输入空间划分（基于共享探针集上的激活模式）的改变，量化客户端与全局模型的功能偏离程度。该指标能有效区分良性异构客户端与产生异常功能偏移的客户端，为联邦学习中的风险感知聚合提供监控工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中，客户端数据异构性（包括良性非独立同分布分布和异常分布偏移/输入）导致全局模型更新不稳定、性能下降，现有方法难以区分良性异构与异常功能偏离。

Method: 提出一种置换不变、可解释的几何度量：在共享探针集上，比较客户端本地训练前后模型对输入空间激活诱导的划分变化，以此量化客户端-全局功能偏离度。

Result: 该信号能有效识别产生异常功能变化的客户端，区分稳定异构客户端与显著偏离全局范式的更新。

Conclusion: 该方法为联邦学习系统提供了一个轻量级的客户端行为监控工具，可支持风险感知的聚合策略。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative training across distributed clients with heterogeneous data, but such heterogeneity often leads to unstable updates and degraded global performance. Moreover, in practical deployments, client updates may deviate from the expected behavior not only due to benign not i.i.d. distributions, but also due to distributional shifts or anomalous inputs, raising concerns about the reliability of the aggregation process. In this work, we propose a lightweight geometric signal to quantify the functional deviation of a client with respect to the global model. Instead of comparing model parameters or gradients, our approach measures how the local training of each client alters the activation-induced partition of the input space, evaluated on a shared probe set. This yields a permutation-invariant, interpretable metric of client--global divergence that captures differences in how data is processed by the model. We show that this signal effectively identifies clients that induce atypical functional changes, distinguishing stable yet heterogeneous clients from those whose updates significantly diverge from the global regime. As a result, the proposed metric provides a simple tool for monitoring client behavior and enabling risk-aware aggregation strategies in federated learning systems.

</details>


### [199] [Adaptive Measurement Allocation for Learning Kernelized SVMs Under Noisy Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22275)
*Artur Miroszewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应测量分配策略，用于从带噪伯努利观测中学习核化支持向量机（SVM）。该方法结合几何敏感性和主动集不稳定性两种互补原则，将测量预算集中分配在最关键的核矩阵区域。理论分析表明自适应分配的收益由核重要性结构的异质性决定，实验证明该方法在支持向量恢复、间隔估计和决策函数精度上显著优于均匀分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统核方法假设精确无噪地获取Gram矩阵，但在量子机器学习等新兴场景中，每个核条目需要从带噪观测中推断，其精度取决于有限的测量预算如何分配。现有方法普遍采用均匀分配，忽略了核化分类器对Gram矩阵的高度非均匀依赖性。

Method: 提出自适应测量分配策略，结合两个互补原理：(i) 几何敏感性——捕捉单个核条目扰动对分类器间隔的影响；(ii) 主动集不稳定性——量化测量噪声引起的支持向量归属离散变化的概率。基于这些信号设计任务感知的分配方案，将测量集中在核矩阵最关键的决策区域。此外提出双系数稳定性准则实现早停。

Result: 在合成数据集上，自适应分配显著提升了支持向量恢复、间隔估计和决策函数精度。在真实数据导出的量子核上表现出与核浓度现象一致的依赖行为。早停策略能在仅使用部分测量成本的情况下实现接近最优的性能。

Conclusion: 自适应分配的收益由核重要性结构的异质性决定，存在自适应策略和均匀策略各自占优的不同 regimes。该工作为带噪核学习中的测量预算优化提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Kernel methods are typically formulated under the assumption of exact, noise-free access to the Gram matrix. However, in emerging settings such as quantum machine learning, each kernel entry must be inferred from noisy observations, and its accuracy depends on how a limited measurement budget is allocated. Despite this, existing approaches overwhelmingly rely on uniform allocation, which equalizes estimator variance but ignores the highly non-uniform dependence of kernelized classifiers on the Gram matrix. In this work, we introduce an adaptive measurement-allocation strategy for learning kernelized Support Vector Machines (SVMs) from noisy Bernoulli observations. Our approach combines two complementary principles: (i) geometric sensitivity, capturing how perturbations of individual kernel entries affect the classifier margin, and (ii) active-set instability, quantifying the probability of discrete changes in support-vector membership induced by measurement noise. These signals define a task-aware allocation scheme that concentrates measurements on the most decision-critical regions of the kernel matrix. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that the benefit of adaptive allocation is governed by the heterogeneity of the induced kernel importance structure, leading to distinct regimes in which adaptive or uniform strategies are preferable. Empirical evaluations on synthetic datasets demonstrate that adaptive allocation significantly improves support-vector recovery, margin estimation, and decision-function accuracy under fixed measurement budgets. A dual-coefficient stability criterion further enables early stopping, achieving near-optimal performance while using only a fraction of the measurement cost. Additional experiments on quantum kernels derived from real-world data reveal a regime-dependent behavior aligned with known phenomena such as kernel concentration. Together...

</details>


### [200] [EmoTrack: Robust Depression Tracking from Counseling Transcripts across Session Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22286)
*Zhaomin Wu,Jiayi Li,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LongCounsel数据集和EmoTrack框架，用于基于文本咨询会话的抑郁症（PHQ-8）追踪。EmoTrack结合LLM提取的临床信号与冻结的语义嵌入，训练症状特异性预测器，并引入跨会话记忆机制，在单会话和多次会话场景下均取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在矛盾：基于微调的方法监督信号丰富但数据稀缺时泛化差，基于prompt的LLM方法数据高效但通常整体处理文本且缺乏对纵向（跨会话）上下文支持。需要一种既能有效利用监督又能处理多会话连续性、且对数据稀缺鲁棒的抑郁追踪方法。

Method: 1) 构建LongCounsel数据集，包含多次会话的咨询记录及会话级PHQ-8标注，支持部分症状披露和跨会话连续性评估。2) 提出EmoTrack框架：利用LLM提取临床相关信号，结合冻结的逐轮语义嵌入，训练症状特异性预测器；当存在历史会话时，通过紧凑的跨会话记忆模块整合历史信息。

Result: 在LongCounsel和DAIC-WOZ上的实验表明：EmoTrack在单次会话基准上取得显著提升，MAE相对降低13.5%（超越DAIC-WOZ最强基线）；在LongCounsel上与最强纵向基线保持竞争力。

Conclusion: EmoTrack通过结合LLM临床信号提取与冻结语义嵌入，并引入跨会话记忆，有效平衡了数据效率与监督利用，在单次和多次会话的抑郁追踪任务中均表现出色，为AI心理健康支持中的会话监测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Text-based counseling is an important interface for AI mental-health support, where transcripts may be used to monitor depression severity and flag sessions requiring timely human review. However, robust PHQ-8 prediction across session regimes remains challenging: fine-tuning-based methods can exploit richer supervision but may generalize poorly under data scarcity, while prompt-based LLM methods are data-efficient but usually treat each transcript holistically and provide limited support for longitudinal context. We study robust depression tracking from counseling transcripts across single-session and multi-session regimes. We introduce LongCounsel, a multi-session counseling dataset with session-level PHQ-8 supervision for evaluating repeated-session tracking under partial symptom disclosure and cross-session continuity. We further propose EmoTrack, a PHQ-8 prediction framework that combines LLM-extracted clinical signals with frozen turn-level semantic embeddings and trains symptom-specific predictors over the resulting transcript representation. When prior sessions are available, EmoTrack can further incorporate them through compact cross-session memory. Experiments on LongCounsel and DAIC-WOZ show that EmoTrack achieves a clear gain on the real single-session benchmark, including a 13.5% relative MAE reduction over the strongest DAIC-WOZ baseline, and remains competitive with the strongest longitudinal baseline on LongCounsel.

</details>


### [201] [One LR Doesn't Fit All: Heavy-Tail Guided Layerwise Learning Rates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22297)
*Di He,Songjun Tu,Keyu Wang,Lu Yin,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR)的逐层自适应学习率方案，基于重尾自正则化(HT-SR)理论，为Transformer不同层分配不同学习率，弱重尾层用大学习率加速训练，强重尾层用小学习率。实验表明LLR在多种架构、优化器和参数规模下可实现1.5倍训练加速，零样本平均准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%，且调优成本低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习中对所有层使用统一学习率的做法忽略了Transformer的结构异质性，限制了大型语言模型的训练效果。

Method: 基于HT-SR理论，通过权重相关矩阵的经验谱密度(ESD)量化各层的重尾性，为弱重尾层分配较大学习率加速训练，强重尾层分配较小学习率，实现逐层差异化学习率配置。

Result: 在LLaMA、GPT-nano等架构，AdamW和Muon优化器，60M-1B参数规模下，LLR实现最高1.5倍训练加速，零样本准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%。

Conclusion: LLR通过自适应逐层学习率促进Transformer各层均衡训练，加速收敛并提升泛化性能，且调优开销低。

Abstract: Learning rate configuration is a fundamental aspect of modern deep learning. The prevailing practice of applying a uniform learning rate across all layers overlooks the structural heterogeneity of Transformers, potentially limiting their effectiveness as the backbone of Large Language Models (LLMs). In this paper, we introduce Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR), an adaptive scheme that assigns distinct learning rates to individual Transformer layers. Our method is grounded in Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory, which characterizes the empirical spectral density (ESD) of weight correlation matrices to quantify heavy-tailedness. Layers with weaker heavy-tailedness are assigned larger learning rates to accelerate their training, while layers with stronger heavy-tailedness receive smaller learning rates. By tailoring learning rates in this manner, LLR promotes balanced training across layers, leading to faster convergence and improved generalization. Extensive experiments across architectures (from LLaMA to GPT-nano), optimizers (AdamW and Muon), and parameter scales (60M-1B) demonstrate that LLR achieves up to 1.5x training speedup and outperforms baselines, notably raising average zero-shot accuracy from 47.09% to 49.02%. A key advantage of LLR is its low tuning overhead: it transfers nearly optimal LR settings directly from the uniform baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/hed-ucas/Layer-wise-Learning-Rate.

</details>


### [202] [SepsisAI Orchestrator: A Containerized and Scalable Platform for Deploying AI Models and Real-Time Monitoring in Early Sepsis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22331)
*Santiago Ospitia,John Sanabria,John Garcia-Henao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SepsisAI-Orchestrator，一个开源模块化平台，通过集成HL7 FHIR风格的CDA预处理、NoSQL存储、容器化LightGBM模型（REST API部署）及Streamlit临床仪表板，解决早期脓毒症检测模型从研究到临床部署的系统性障碍。在k6负载测试中（50-1000并发用户），发现副本数需匹配物理CPU线程数：12线程CPU上从3副本扩至12副本使p95延迟降低57.3%（3.3s→1.41s）并消除请求失败，而过量扩至24/48副本因调度竞争导致性能下降，首次量化了临床AI推理负载的U型扩展行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习模型虽然预测效果优异，但受限于异构数据表示、缺乏标准化部署流程以及研究原型与医院环境并发性、延迟要求不匹配等系统级障碍，难以转化为实际床旁应用。

Method: 构建开放源码模块化平台，整合HL7 FHIR风格的CDA数据预处理、NoSQL存储、容器化LightGBM分类器（通过REST API服务）和Streamlit临床仪表板，使用Docker和Kubernetes编排。采用k6工具模拟50-1000并发虚拟用户进行负载测试，系统性地改变副本数（3/6/12/24/48）并测量p95延迟和请求失败率。

Result: 在12线程CPU上，副本数从3扩至12时p95延迟从3.3s降至1.41s（降幅57.3%），请求失败完全消除；但过度扩至24或48副本时性能反而下降。首次量化了临床AI推理工作负载中副本数与延迟之间的U型缩放关系。

Conclusion: 该平台成功填补了脓毒症检测模型从研究到部署的工程鸿沟，并揭示了临床AI推理负载中副本数需与物理CPU线程数匹配的关键发现。未进行前瞻性临床验证，代码已开源。

Abstract: Despite strong predictive results in the clinical machine learning literature, the translation of these models into bedside use remains limited by systems-level barriers: heterogeneous data representations, the absence of standardized deployment workflows, and a mismatch between research prototypes and the concurrency and latency requirements of hospital environments. We present the SepsisAI-Orchestrator, an open-source modular platform that addresses this deployment gap for early sepsis detection. The platform integrates HL7 FHIR-inspired Clinical Document Architecture (CDA) preprocessing, NoSQL storage, a containerized LightGBM classifier served via REST APIs, and a Streamlit clinical dashboard, orchestrated with Docker and Kubernetes. A previously validated LightGBM model (F1 0.87-0.94 on PhysioNet 2019) is reused without modification; the contribution lies in the surrounding infrastructure and its empirical characterization under load. Using k6 with 50-1000 concurrent virtual users, we find that replica count must be matched to the physical CPU thread count of the host: scaling from 3 to 12 replicas on a 12-thread CPU reduces p95 latency from 3.3s to 1.41s (57.3% reduction) and eliminates all request failures, while over-provisioning to 24 or 48 replicas degrades performance due to scheduler contention. To our knowledge this U-shaped scaling behavior has not been quantified previously for clinical AI inference workloads. We do not claim prospective clinical validation. Source code and deployment manifests are available at https://github.com/nucleusai/sepsisai-orchestrator.

</details>


### [203] [Learning Causal Orderings for In-Context Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22335)
*Sascha Xu,Sarah Mameche,Jilles Vreeken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabOrder模型，通过因果顺序约束的注意力机制同时推断和执行表格数据的因果结构，在分布偏移和干预下实现更可靠的预测与插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文学习主要依赖相关结构，在分布偏移或干预下不可靠；因果发现方法虽能发现因果结构，但与预测架构脱节。需要弥合这两种视角。

Method: TabOrder采用因果顺序约束的注意力机制，仅基于目标变量在所学因果顺序下的前序特征进行预测；通过无监督的似然目标学习最优变量排序，并研究缺失数据与因果方向识别的交互。

Result: TabOrder能恢复准确的变量排序，有效完成预测和插补任务，并在真实生物学数据干预下提供有价值的洞察。

Conclusion: 将因果结构推断与预测架构结合是可行的，TabOrder在分布偏移下比依赖相关结构的方法更可靠，且能处理缺失数据等实际问题。

Abstract: In-context learning for tabular data sets strong predictive standards in observational settings; it however primarily relies on correlational structure, which becomes unreliable under distribution shift or intervention. While established methods to discover causal structure exist, they are often focused on structure identifiability and decoupled from the predictive architectures that could benefit from them.
  To bridge these perspectives, we study how to simultaneously infer and enforce causal structure in the form of topological variable orderings into tabular prediction. Unlike standard architectures, our model TabOrder uses causal order-constrained attention, basing predictions only on features that precede a target under a learned causal order. Similar to causal discovery methods, TabOrder learns the optimal variable ordering in an unsupervised manner through a likelihood-based objective. We justify this choice under standard functional model classes and also study how sample missingness, a common challenge in tabular data, interacts with causal direction identification. Empirically, we confirm that TabOrder recovers accurate variable orderings while addressing prediction and imputation tasks, as well as gives insight into real-world biological data under intervention.

</details>


### [204] [From Snapshots to Trajectories: Learning Single-Cell Gene Expression Dynamics via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22340)
*Siyu Pu,Qingqing Long,Xiaohan Huang,Haotian Chen,Jiajia Wang,Meng Xiao,Xiao Luo,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou,Xuezhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出scFM，一种基于耦合条件流匹配的潜变量生成框架，用于解决单细胞RNA测序时序数据中快照未配对和长时程预测漂移两大挑战，通过熵正则化最优传输构建软加权流匹配目标、学习双向速度场并利用一致性优化耦合、引入分布级对齐和潜动态正则化，在真实数据集上实现了更优的时间插值与外推性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞RNA测序时序数据仅在少数离散时间点采集为未配对的快照，现有方法存在两大挑战：(i) 未配对快照导致相邻时间点间的局部转移模糊不清，监督信号不稳定；(ii) 长时程预测依赖反复积分，微小建模误差累积导致分布漂移。

Method: 提出scFM框架：(1) 计算相邻快照间的熵正则化OT耦合，构建软加权流匹配目标以学习时变速度场；(2) 学习双向速度场，利用其一致性改进耦合，增强稀疏监督下的时间连贯性；(3) 引入分布级对齐和潜动态正则化来锚定长程 rollout，缓解漂移。

Result: 在真实时序scRNA-seq数据集上，scFM在时间插值与外推的分布预测性能上持续提升，轨迹重建更准确，中间时间点缺失时仍能生成时间连贯的可视化结果。

Conclusion: scFM能够更忠实地恢复潜在的时序基因表达动态，有效解决了未配对快照和长时程预测漂移问题。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) provides high-dimensional profiles of cellular states, enabling data-driven modeling of cellular dynamics over time. In practice, time-resolved scRNA-seq is collected at only a few discrete time points as unpaired snapshot populations, leaving substantial temporal gaps. This motivates trajectory inference at unmeasured time points. Existing methods mainly follow two directions, optimal-transport (OT) alignment provides distribution-level matching between observed snapshots, while continuous-time generative models support forecasting via learned dynamics. However, two challenges remain: (i) unpaired snapshots render local transitions between adjacent time points ambiguous, leading to unstable supervision; and (ii) long-horizon prediction relies on repeated integration, where small modeling errors compound and cause distribution drift. To address these challenges, we propose single-cell Flow Matching (scFM), a latent generative framework based on coupling-conditioned flow matching. First, we compute entropically regularized OT couplings between adjacent snapshots and use them to construct soft, weighted flow-matching targets for learning time-dependent velocity fields. Second, we learn bidirectional velocity fields and leverage their consistency to refine couplings and improve temporal coherence under sparse supervision. Third, we introduce distribution-level alignment and latent dynamic regularization to anchor long rollouts and mitigate drift. Experiments on real-world time-series scRNA-seq datasets show that scFM consistently improves distributional prediction performance for both temporal interpolation and extrapolation. Moreover, scFM yields more accurate trajectory reconstruction and temporally coherent visualizations where intermediate time points are absent, indicating a more faithful recovery of underlying temporal gene expression dynamics.

</details>


### [205] [VeriScale: Adversarial Test-Suite Scaling for Verifiable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22368)
*Yifan Bai,Xiaoyang Liu,Zihao Mou,Guihong Wang,Jian Yu,Shuhan Xie,Yantao Li,Yangyu Zhang,Jingwei Liang,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeriScale是一个通过对抗性实现驱动的新型框架，用于扩增和精简测试套件，以构建更高质量的代码验证基准。基于该框架构建的VerinaPlus（测试套件扩展83倍）和VerinaLite（轻量级14倍变体）在8个LLM上的实验表明，新基准能揭示原基准隐藏的模型弱点，显著降低SpecGen和CodeGen任务得分，同时VerinaLite以较低评估成本保持判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准受限于正负测试用例的数量和质量，导致在评估LLM生成规范和实现的能力时出现高估。

Method: 提出VeriScale框架，包含两个阶段：测试套件扩增（构建多样且具有挑战性的测试用例）和测试套件精简（蒸馏为紧凑但具判别力的套件）。在Verina基准上实例化得到VerinaPlus（扩增83倍）和VerinaLite（精简14倍）。

Result: 在8个SOTA LLM上的实验表明，VerinaPlus揭示了原基准隐藏的模型弱点，导致SpecGen和CodeGen任务得分大幅下降；VerinaLite在降低评估成本的同时保持了判别能力。

Conclusion: VeriScale框架能够构建更高质量的代码验证基准，更准确地评估LLM在代码规范生成和代码生成任务上的真实能力。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for software engineering, constructing high-quality benchmarks is crucial for evaluating not just the functional correctness, but also the formal verifiability of generated code. However, existing benchmarks are limited by the quantity and quality of positive and negative test cases, leading to an overestimation of model capabilities in generating specifications and implementations. To address this, we propose VeriScale, a novel framework driven by the adversarial implementations. It consists of two stages: test-suite expansion to construct diverse and challenging test cases, and test-suite reduction to distill them into compact yet discriminative suites. While VeriScale is general, we instantiate it on Verina to construct VerinaPlus, which expands the original test suites by over 83$\times$, and VerinaLite, a lightweight 14$\times$ variant. Our experiments across eight state-of-the-art LLMs demonstrate that VerinaPlus exposes substantial model weaknesses hidden by the original benchmark, evidenced by sharp score drops on both SpecGen and CodeGen tasks, whereas VerinaLite maintains this discriminative power at a fraction of the evaluation cost. The enhanced benchmarks and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/XiaoyangLiu-sjtu/VeriScale.

</details>


### [206] [ASAP: Attention Sink Anchored Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22372)
*Jaehyuk Lee,Hanyoung Kim,Yanggee Kim,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ASAP框架，通过将注意力汇现象转化为特征，利用径向扩散聚类和转移权重池化进行令牌剪枝，在无需训练的情况下提升ViT推理速度最高48%并保持或提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformer在高分辨率下自注意力机制的计算复杂度呈平方级增长，现有基于局部度量（如单层注意力分数）的令牌缩减方法容易受到注意力汇现象影响，导致无信息令牌被保留而显著前景令牌被丢弃。

Method: 将ViT信息流建模为惰性随机游走，计算令牌到注意力汇的扩散距离，通过径向扩散聚类划分令牌，并利用转移权重池化单次压缩背景冗余。

Result: 在图像、视频和视觉-语言任务上，ASAP在加速高达48%的同时保持甚至超越基线精度，优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: ASAP是一种无需训练的高效令牌剪枝框架，通过重新利用注意力汇特征解决了现有方法的根本缺陷，实现了速度与精度的双赢。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) face severe computational bottlenecks due to the quadratic complexity of self-attention at high resolutions. Existing token reduction methods rely on local metrics - such as single-layer attention scores - that are inherently vulnerable to the attention sink phenomenon, where uninformative tokens are paradoxically preserved over salient foreground objects. We propose ASAP (Attention Sink Anchored Pruning), a training-free framework that recasts this sink as a feature. Modeling ViT information flow as a Lazy Random Walk, ASAP identifies the sink as a dominant accumulator of probability mass. By computing the diffusion distance to the sink within the cumulative transition matrix, ASAP partitions tokens via Radial Diffusion Clustering and compresses background redundancy through Transition Weight Pooling in a single shot. Extensive experiments across image, video, and vision-language tasks demonstrate ASAP outperforms state-of-the-art methods, accelerating throughput by up to 48% while maintaining - or even exceeding - baseline accuracy.

</details>


### [207] [Target-Aligned Bellman Backup for Cross-domain Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22376)
*Wei Liu,Ting Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出目标对齐贝尔曼备份（TABB）方法，通过在跨域离线强化学习中基于贝尔曼目标对齐度而非表面相似性来选择源域迁移数据，在目标域数据极度有限的场景下持续取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域离线强化学习工作通常基于过渡相似性（transition-level similarity）来评估源域数据的可迁移性，但过渡相似性并不保证长期回报的一致性——即使在视觉或动态上相似的过渡也可能在目标域导致截然不同的结果，从而误导策略学习并降低性能。

Method: TABB方法重新审视策略学习的基本目标，提出基于源域过渡与目标域贝尔曼目标的对齐程度（而非表面过渡相似性）来评估其可迁移性；通过衡量源域数据对目标域中准确贝尔曼目标估计的贡献，选择性利用源域数据进行策略学习。

Result: 在多种跨域离线强化学习场景下，且目标域数据极其有限时，TABB方法持续取得优异性能。

Conclusion: 基于贝尔曼目标对齐的迁移数据选择策略优于基于表面过渡相似性的方法，能够更有效地在跨域离线强化学习中利用源域数据提升目标域策略学习。

Abstract: Cross-domain offline reinforcement learning (CDRL) aims to improve policy learning in a target domain by leveraging data collected from a source domain. Existing works typically assess the transferability of source-domain data by measuring its similarity to target-domain transitions, and implicitly perform transition-level selection. Transitions that are considered similar are assigned higher weights or rewards, while dissimilar ones are down-weighted. However, transition-level similarity does not necessarily imply consistency in long-term returns. Even visually or dynamically similar transitions may lead to significantly different outcomes in the target domain, which can mislead policy learning and degrade performance. To address this issue, we revisit the fundamental objective of policy learning. Since policy optimization ultimately relies on Bellman targets to evaluate the quality of decisions, we propose to assess the transferability of source-domain transitions based on their alignment with target-domain Bellman targets, rather than superficial transition similarity. Based on this insight, we propose a method termed Target-Aligned Bellman Backup (TABB), which selectively leverages source-domain data by measuring their contribution to accurate Bellman target estimation in the target domain. We evaluate TABB across a broad range of cross-domain offline RL settings with highly limited target-domain data. Experimental results show that TABB consistently achieves strong performance.

</details>


### [208] [Efficient Higher-order Subgraph Attribution via Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22385)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Grégoire Montavon,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出线性时间算法，将GNN-LRP的子图归因复杂度从指数级降至线性，并通过消息传递技术实现高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN-LRP虽然能有效解释GNN，但其子图归因需要对指数级数量的路径进行求和，计算复杂度高，限制了其可扩展性和实用性。

Method: 提出基于消息传递的线性时间算法，利用分配律直接计算高阶解释所需的量，避免了显式枚举所有路径，并进一步扩展算法以融入邻域图特征。

Result: 实验证明所提算法实现了显著的加速，并且新的广义子图归因方法具有良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: GNN-LRP的子图归因可以避免指数复杂度，通过线性时间算法实现更高效的解释，且可扩展到考虑邻域特征的广义归因。

Abstract: Explaining graph neural networks (GNNs) has become more and more important recently. Higher-order interpretation schemes, such as GNN-LRP (layer-wise relevance propagation for GNN), emerged as powerful tools for unraveling how different features interact thereby contributing to explaining GNNs. GNN-LRP gives a relevance attribution of walks between nodes at each layer, and the subgraph attribution is expressed as a sum over exponentially many such walks. In this work, we demonstrate that such exponential complexity can be avoided. In particular, we propose novel algorithms that enable to attribute subgraphs with GNN-LRP in linear-time (w.r.t. the network depth). Our algorithms are derived via message passing techniques that make use of the distributive property, thereby directly computing quantities for higher-order explanations. We further adapt our efficient algorithms to compute a generalization of subgraph attributions that also takes into account the neighboring graph features. Experimental results show the significant acceleration of the proposed algorithms and demonstrate the high usefulness and scalability of our novel generalized subgraph attribution method.

</details>


### [209] [Hybrid Kolmogorov-Arnold Network and XGBoost Framework for Week-Ahead Price Forecasting in Australia's National Electricity Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22387)
*Houxuan Zhou,Sriram Prasad,Chenghao Huang,Jiajie Feng,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合KAN（Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks）与XGBoost的混合框架用于澳大利亚国家电力市场（NEM）的周前电价预测，在真实数据上相比SARIMAX、LSTM、独立KAN和XGBoost等基准方法，MAE降低约12%（相对XGBoost）至50%以上（相对朴素基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电价预测因高波动性、非线性动态和频繁极端价格尖峰而极具挑战，尤其是澳大利亚NEM市场受高比例可再生能源渗透进一步加剧了不确定性，现有方法在捕捉长期依赖与短期波动方面存在不足。

Method: 提出KAN+XGBoost混合框架：利用KAN的全局非线性表示能力捕捉长期依赖，结合XGBoost的局部鲁棒性处理短期波动；采用扩展窗口评估策略在真实NEM数据上进行实验。

Result: 所提模型在所有基准方法中表现最优：MAE相比XGBoost降低约12%，相比朴素基线降低超过50%；优于SARIMAX、LSTM、独立KAN和独立XGBoost。

Conclusion: 混合学习策略（KAN与XGBoost结合）为高动态电力市场中的电价预测提供了有效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate electricity price forecasting (EPF) is essential for market participants to support operational planning and risk management, yet remains challenging due to strong volatility, nonlinear dynamics, and frequent extreme price spikes. These challenges are particularly pronounced in the Australian National Electricity Market (NEM), where high renewable penetration further increases uncertainty. This paper investigates week-ahead electricity price forecasting and proposes a hybrid KAN+XGBoost framework that integrates Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN) with tree-based learning. The proposed approach combines the global nonlinear representation capability of KAN with the local robustness of XGBoost to capture both long-term dependencies and short-term price fluctuations. Experiments are conducted on real-world NEM data using an expanding window evaluation strategy. The results demonstrate that the proposed model outperforms benchmark methods, including SARIMAX, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), standalone KAN, and XGBoost, reducing MAE by approximately 12% compared to XGBoost and by over 50% compared to a naive baseline. The results suggest that hybrid learning strategies provide an effective and robust solution for electricity price forecasting in highly dynamic electricity markets.

</details>


### [210] [Cross-Species RSA Reveals Conserved Early Visual Alignment but Divergent Higher-Area Rankings Across Human fMRI and Macaque Electrophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22401)
*Nils Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了五种学习规则（BP、FA、PC、STDP、随机基线）训练的CNN与猕猴电生理数据的对齐程度，发现：所有模型在猕猴早期视觉皮层（V1/V2）的对齐度高于人类fMRI；STDP和PC在V1/V2表现最优；在高级视觉区IT，学习规则排序无跨物种相关性，但预训练ResNet-50显著优于所有定制CNN，表明IT对齐受模型容量和训练数据限制而非学习规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证学习规则与大脑对齐的关系是否具有跨物种普遍性，即先前在人类V1 fMRI中发现的结论能否推广到猕猴电生理数据。

Method: 使用与人类研究相同权重的CNN模型（BP、FA、PC、STDP、随机基线），对猕猴电生理数据（MajajHong2015的V4/IT和FreemanZiemba2013的V1/V2）进行表征相似性分析（RSA），并加入预训练ResNet-50对比。

Result: (1)所有模型在猕猴V1/V2对齐度（rho=0.15-0.30）高于人类fMRI（rho=0.01-0.08）；(2)STDP和PC在猕猴V1/V2最佳（rho≈0.30和0.28）；(3)IT层学习规则排序跨物种无相关性（Kendall's tau=0.00, p=1.00）；(4)预训练ResNet-50在猕猴IT达rho=0.25，远超定制CNN（0.07-0.14）。

Conclusion: 早期视觉对齐具有跨物种稳健性，高级视觉区对齐受模型容量和刺激领域影响而非学习规则。IT跨物种无相关性的结论受限于统计功效（n=5）和刺激集差异。

Abstract: Does the relationship between learning rules and brain alignment generalize across species? We extend our prior finding that untrained CNNs match backpropagation at human V1 by testing the same five learning rules against macaque electrophysiology. The rules are backpropagation (BP), feedback alignment (FA), predictive coding (PC), spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), and an untrained random-weights baseline. The macaque data come from two datasets: MajajHong2015 (V4/IT, 3,200 stimulus presentations, 88/168 neurons) and FreemanZiemba2013 (V1/V2, 135 stimuli, 102/103 neurons). Using RSA with identical model weights from our human study, we find: (1) all models achieve higher alignment with macaque early visual cortex (rho = 0.15-0.30 at V1/V2) than with human fMRI (rho = 0.01-0.08), consistent with the higher signal-to-noise ratio of electrophysiology; (2) STDP and PC produce the highest macaque V1/V2 alignment (rho ~ 0.30 and 0.28), consistent with their leading position among trained rules in human V1; (3) at IT, learning rule rankings show no detectable correlation across species (Kendall's tau = 0.00, p = 1.00), though this null result is expected given that n = 5 provides power only at tau = +/-1.0, and is further confounded by stimulus set differences; (4) a pretrained ResNet-50 (ImageNet) achieves rho = 0.25 at macaque IT, substantially above all custom CNN conditions (rho = 0.07-0.14), suggesting IT alignment is limited by model capacity and training data rather than by the learning rule. Noise ceilings, multi-seed variability (5 seeds), and a stimulus-control analysis are reported. These results demonstrate that early visual alignment is robust across species, while higher-area alignment is modulated by model capacity and stimulus domain.

</details>


### [211] [AMUSE: Anytime Muon with Stable Gradient Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22432)
*Jueun Kim,Baekrok Shin,Jihun Yun,Beomhan Baek,Minhak Song,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMUSE方法，结合Muon的正交化加速和Schedule-Free的迭代平均稳定效果，通过时变插值系数实现无学习率调度的随时训练，在视觉任务和LLM预训练中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon虽然通过正交化动量加速了平坦子空间（河流）的训练进度，但同时也放大了主导方向的噪声导致振荡轨迹；Schedule-Free优化通过迭代平均抑制了谷壁振荡。作者希望将两者的优势结合，实现既快速又稳定的优化。

Method: 提出AMUSE（Anytime MUon with Stable gradient Evaluation），使用时变插值系数：初始阶段在快速的Muon序列附近评估梯度以快速适应，然后逐渐向稳定的平均序列偏移以抑制谷壁振荡，无需学习率调度。

Result: 在视觉任务和大语言模型预训练中，AMUSE consistently（持续地）改进了性能-迭代Pareto前沿，优于Schedule-Free AdamW和Muon。

Conclusion: AMUSE成功融合了Muon的快速河床推进能力与Schedule-Free的稳定化效果，实现了无学习率调度的随时训练，验证了在河流-山谷损失景观下正交化与平均化结合的有效性。

Abstract: Modern deep learning commonly relies on AdamW with prescribed learning rate schedules, but recent works challenge both components: Schedule-Free optimization removes explicit schedules via iterate averaging, and Muon improves the update geometry by orthogonalizing momentum for matrix parameters. Despite Muon's strong empirical performance, its underlying mechanism remains partially understood. We study Muon through the river-valley loss landscape, where useful training progress occurs along a flat, low-curvature bulk subspace (the river), while high-curvature dominant directions form steep valley walls that induce oscillations. We empirically show that while Muon's orthogonalization accelerates river progress by increasing the bulk component, it also amplifies dominant-direction noise, causing oscillatory trajectories. Building on this, we propose Anytime MUon with Stable gradient Evaluation (AMUSE), which integrates Muon's rapid bulk progress with the stabilizing effect of Schedule-Free averaging. AMUSE uses a time-varying interpolation coefficient that initially evaluates gradients near the fast Muon sequence for rapid adaptation, then gradually shifts toward the stable averaged sequence to suppress valley-wall oscillations. As a result, AMUSE requires no learning rate schedules and supports anytime training. Across vision tasks and large language model pretraining, AMUSE consistently improves the performance-iteration Pareto frontier over (Schedule-Free) AdamW and Muon.

</details>


### [212] [Understanding Multimodal Failure in Action-Chunking Behavioral Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22493)
*Lorenzo Mazza,Massimiliano Datres,Ariel Rodriguez,Sebastian Bodenstedt,Gitta Kutyniok,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了行为克隆中同一观测对应多个有效动作的多模态问题，分析了潜变量策略和动作空间生成策略两种参数化方式在多模态场景下的失效机制，并通过实验验证了这些机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆在观测对应多个有效动作时（多模态场景）表现困难，需要理解不同多模态参数化方法为何以及如何失效。

Method: 从理论上分析了两种多模态参数化方法：（1）潜变量策略中后验-先验正则化对部署采样的影响；（2）动作空间生成策略中基空间到动作空间映射的Lipschitz常数对多模态覆盖能力的约束。并在合成多模态任务和机器人仿真基准上进行了实验验证。

Result: 过度正则化会消除区分演示模态所需的动作条件信息；减少正则化可保留模态信息，但依赖先验覆盖相关潜变量区域。动作空间生成策略中，小Lipschitz常数的映射无法给多个分离模态分配显著概率，覆盖多模态需要基空间的尖锐变换或动作空间的桥接区域。

Conclusion: 不同多模态参数化方法各有不同的失效模式，需要根据具体场景设计合适的策略来应对多模态行为克隆问题。

Abstract: Behavioral cloning becomes difficult when the same observation admits several valid actions. We study this problem for action-chunking policies and show that different multimodal parameterizations fail in different ways. For latent-variable policies, posterior-prior regularization makes deployment-time sampling more reliable, but excessive regularization removes the action-conditioned information needed to distinguish demonstrated modes. Reducing this regularization can preserve mode information, but then success depends on whether the prior covers the relevant latent regions. For action-space generative policies, multimodality is constrained by the smoothness of the base-to-action transport: a map with small Lipschitz constant cannot assign substantial probability to many well-separated modes. Covering many modes therefore requires either sharp transitions in base space or off-support bridge regions in action space. Experiments on synthetic multimodal tasks and robotic simulation benchmarks support these mechanisms.

</details>


### [213] [The Neural Compiler: Program-to-Network Translation for Hybrid Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22498)
*Lucas Sheneman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Neural Compiler，将类 Scheme 表达式语言编写的程序编译为可微分的 PyTorch 模块，精确编码已知物理方程，同时支持与学习组件混合建模，实现符号到可微分模块的自动编译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习中常需将已知物理规律与数据驱动的未知参数/修正项结合，现有方法要么忽略已知结构，要么将其作为软惩罚编码，要么为每个方程手工编写 PyTorch 代码，缺乏系统化、自动化的编译方案。

Method: 设计一种一阶类 Scheme 表达式语言，将其程序编译为冻结的、可微分的 PyTorch 模块，编译结果与源程序浮点精度一致，并通过 autograd 提供梯度；编译模块精确编码已知物理，学习组件建模未知残差。

Result: 在 Feynman 物理方程、Lotka-Volterra、阻尼摆、一维热方程、三维矢量力学及组合泛化六个实验域评估。单方程精度与手写 PyTorch 实现数值一致；1-4个可训练参数即可恢复物理常数（误差<1%），而标准 PINN 基线（>8500参数）误差7-93%；编译模块组合零误差，神经近似在深层组合链中累积大误差。

Conclusion: 编译器的主要价值不在于比手写方程精度更高，而在于系统性可组合性：能从符号规约自动生成正确的可微分模块，无需手写每个方程。支持 51 种原语操作（含向量/矩阵代数），支持 PDE 离散化和混合科学模型。字符串输入-模块输出的接口也为大语言模型将科学描述翻译为可执行可微分模块提供了自然目标。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning often requires combining known physics with unknown parameters or correction terms learned from data. Existing approaches either ignore known structure, encode it as a soft penalty, or require hand-written PyTorch code for each equation. We present The Neural Compiler, a system that translates programs written in a first-order Scheme-like expression language into frozen, differentiable PyTorch modules. These modules match the source program to floating-point precision and provide gradients through autograd. In hybrid models, the compiled module encodes known physics exactly while learned components model the unknown remainder. We evaluate the compiler across six experiment domains: Feynman physics equations, Lotka-Volterra dynamics, a damped pendulum, a one-dimensional heat equation, three-dimensional vector mechanics, and compositional generalization. Compiled modules match hand-coded PyTorch implementations numerically for single equations, showing no accuracy loss from compilation. With only 1 to 4 trainable parameters, compiled models recover physical constants to less than 1 percent error in most cases, while standard PINN baselines with more than 8500 parameters show 7 to 93 percent error. Compiled modules also compose with zero error, while neural approximations can accumulate large errors in deep composition chains. The main value of the compiler is not improved accuracy over hand-coded equations, but systematic composability: it generates correct, differentiable modules from symbolic specifications without rewriting each equation by hand. The system supports 51 primitive operations, including vector and matrix algebra, enabling PDE discretizations and hybrid scientific models. This string-in, module-out interface also provides a natural target for large language models that translate scientific descriptions into executable differentiable modules.

</details>


### [214] [Lost in Tokenization: Fundamental Trade-offs in Graph Tokenization for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22471)
*Maya Bechler-Speicher,Gilad Yehudai,Gil Harari,Clayton Sanford,Amir Globerson,Joan Bruna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图Transformer中不同tokenization（谱、随机游走、邻接）对模型表达能力的影响，证明不同的tokenization会导致不同的深度需求，某些tokenization会丢失信息或产生病态条件，且有限深度的Transformer无法在不同tokenization族之间高效转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformers已成为图学习的核心架构，但其应用需要先选择一种tokenization（图到令牌的映射），而这一选择对Transformer表达能力的影响尚不明确。本文旨在系统研究不同tokenization如何影响图Transformer的表达能力和效率。

Method: 通过理论分析证明不同tokenization（谱、随机游走、邻接）的数学性质，包括信息损失、条件数等；建立下界和不可能性结果，证明有限深度Transformer无法在不同tokenization族之间转换；并用合成和真实任务进行实验验证。
