Result: 不同的tokenization会导致不同的深度需求；随机游走tokenization是信息有损的；谱tokenization虽无损但对局部任务病态；有限深度Transformer无法在不同tokenization族之间转换；不同任务偏好不同的结构视图，组合互补tokenization可以提升性能。

Conclusion: Tokenization是图Transformer表达力的基础组成部分，不同tokenization适用于不同任务，组合多种tokenization有助于利用不同表示中的信号。

Abstract: Transformers have become a central architecture for graph learning, but their application to graphs requires first choosing a tokenization: a graph-to-token map that determines which structural information is exposed at the input. In this work, we show that this choice is a fundamental component of transformer expressivity. We examine three tokenizations that serve as building blocks for many existing graph tokenizations: spectral, random-walk, and adjacency tokenizations. We prove that different tokenizations induce distinct depth regimes: the same graph computation may be realizable by a shallow transformer under one tokenization, while requiring substantially larger depth under another. For example, we prove that random-walk tokenization is lossy for any walk length, making it impossible in general to recover the graph from it, and that while spectral tokenization is lossless, it is ill-conditioned for local tasks. We further show that although both random-walk and spectral tokenizations are derived from adjacency information, it is impossible for a limited-depth transformer to convert between tokenization families in general. In particular, we establish lower bounds and impossibility results showing that unfavorable tokenizations may preclude the efficient recovery of more suitable structural representations. Finally, we complement our theory with controlled experiments on synthetic and real-world tasks, validating the predicted separations and showing that different tasks favor different structural views, and combining complementary tokenizations allows the transformer to leverage distinct signals from each representation.

</details>


### [215] [Abstraction for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22711)
*Clarisse Wibault,Alexander Goldie,Antonio Villares,Maike Osborne,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在离线目标条件强化学习中，层次策略不仅能够通过时间抽象减少规划视野，还能通过引入相对化选项和分层表示实现绝对抽象，从而跨相似状态-目标对复用经验，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马尔可夫决策过程在现实世界目标条件强化学习中常因对称性和共享结构存在显著冗余，现有方法未充分利用这种结构信息来提升样本效率。

Method: 引入相对化选项（relativised options）以及不同层级使用不同表示的方法，使智能体能够在状态空间中跨相似上下文复用经验，并基于此框架提出两种简单算法。

Result: 实验表明，这种归纳偏置在离线目标条件强化学习中显著提升了性能。

Conclusion: 层次策略的绝对抽象能力是离线GCRL中一个重要但被忽视的维度，相对化选项能有效利用MDP中的冗余结构提升学习效率。

Abstract: Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) often exhibit significant redundancy due to symmetries and shared structure across state-goal pairs in real-world Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (GCRL). While hierarchical policies have been motivated for horizon reduction via temporal abstraction in offline GCRL, we demonstrate that hierarchy also enables absolute abstraction. By introducing relativised options as well as distinct representations for different levels of the hierarchy, we demonstrate how an agent can reuse experience across similar contexts of the state-space. Based on this framework, we introduce two simple algorithms for learning relativised options and abstracting from the absolute frame of reference. Our experiments show that such inductive biases significantly improve performance in offline GCRL.

</details>


### [216] [The Signal in the Noise: OOD Detection Through Goodness-of-Fit Testing in Factorised Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22496)
*Philipp Bomatter,Jack Geary,Henry Gouk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SITN方法，利用连续归一化流的微分同胚和质量保持特性，通过分析噪声空间中的异常模式来检测分布外样本，无需OOD数据且计算开销小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型在分布外（OOD）检测中虽然理论上很自然，但其分配的对数似然作为OOD指标并不可靠，需要新的方法来改进OOD检测。

Method: 利用连续归一化流（continuous normalising flows）的微分同胚（diffeomorphic）和质量保持（mass-preserving）特性，提出Signal in the Noise (SITN)方法，在噪声空间中检测异常模式，无需OOD数据，单样本级别检测，计算开销极小，且能严格控制假阳性率。

Result: 通过标准基准测试和合成扰动实验，验证了SITN方法的有效性，并且不存在基于似然方法固有的复杂度偏差（complexity bias）。

Conclusion: SITN是一种高效、可控且无需OOD数据的OOD检测方法，克服了传统似然方法的局限性。

Abstract: Deep generative models offer a natural foundation for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, yet prior work has shown that their assigned likelihoods are notoriously unreliable indicators for in- vs out-of-distribution data. In this paper, we address this problem by leveraging the diffeomorphic and mass-preserving properties of continuous normalising flows. Our analysis shows that OOD samples are mapped to noise samples that are highly atypical under the noise prior in ways not captured by the likelihood. Based on this observation, we propose a new method -- Signal in the Noise (SITN) -- for OOD detection on the single-sample level. SITN requires no access to OOD data, incurs minimal computational overhead, and provides strict control of false positive rates. Comprehensive evaluations through standard benchmarks and synthetic perturbations highlight the method's effectiveness and the absence of the complexity bias inherent to likelihood-based methods.

</details>


### [217] [The Value of Covariance Matching in Gaussian DDPMs and the Lanczos Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22723)
*Md Sahil Akhtar,Aymane El Gadarri,Vivek F. Farias,Adam D. Jozefiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS），通过匹配全后验协方差将DDPM的路径KL散度从O(1/T)降至O(1/T^2)，仅需少量（如3次）Jacobian-向量乘积即可实现优于对角协方差基线的样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准各向同性逆协方差在路径KL散度上存在不可避免的Ω(1/T)误差，而匹配全后验协方差理论上可突破此界限，但全协方差匹配因计算和存储成本过高而难以实用。

Method: 提出Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS），一种无训练、无矩阵的方法：利用后验均值的Jacobian-向量积实现协方差-向量积，通过Lanczos迭代生成最优逆协方差的采样近似，避免显式存储稠密协方差矩阵或训练辅助协方差模型。

Result: 理论证明LGS近似误差随Lanczos步数指数衰减；实验上仅用3步Lanczos即可在标准图像基准上超越OCM-DDPM等强对角协方差基线。

Conclusion: 全协方差匹配在理论上价值显著且在实践中（通过LGS）可行，为快速DDPM采样提供了新范式。

Abstract: A central error measure in Gaussian DDPMs is the path-space KL divergence between the exact reverse chain and the learned Gaussian reverse process. This quantity is especially relevant for procedures such as classifier guidance, which perturb the entire reverse trajectory rather than only the terminal sample. Prior analyses show that standard isotropic reverse covariances suffer an unavoidable $Ω(1/T)$ path-KL error as the number of denoising steps $T$ grows. We show that matching the full posterior covariance breaks this barrier, yielding an order-wise improvement that reduces the path KL to $O(1/T^2)$. To make full covariance matching practical, we introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method for sampling from the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products, which are available through Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense covariance storage and auxiliary covariance models. We prove that LGS approximation error decays exponentially in the number of Lanczos steps, where each Lanczos step requires a single Jacobian-vector product. Empirically, using only just three such steps improves sample quality over strong diagonal-covariance baselines, including OCM-DDPM, across standard image benchmarks. This identifies full covariance matching as both theoretically valuable and practically accessible for fast DDPM sampling.

</details>


### [218] [Generative Modeling by Value-Driven Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22507)
*Pablo Moreno-Muñoz,Adrian Müller,Gergely Neu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散时间随机控制理论的生成建模新框架，通过线性规划对偶形式求解最优价值函数，开发了无仿真的原始-对偶算法来学习价值驱动传输（VDT）策略，该策略具有直路径、快速鲁棒模拟等优势，并在多种实验中展现了良好的性能和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流、扩散或Schrödinger桥的生成建模方法存在模拟路径弯曲、计算效率低等问题，亟需一种能生成直路径、可快速鲁棒模拟且易于集成条件生成等功能的生成框架。

Method: 将测度传输问题建模为离散时间随机控制问题，转化为线性规划形式，利用对偶变量（最优价值函数）直接编码最优控制策略；设计无仿真的原始-对偶算法来近似求解最优价值函数，进而得到价值驱动传输（VDT）策略。

Result: VDT策略生成直传输路径，可快速鲁棒模拟；支持条件生成、无分类器引导、非配对数据翻译等扩展功能；在多项实验中表现出强性能和良好可扩展性。

Conclusion: VDT框架在生成建模方面具有显著优势，可作为扩散模型和流模型的强有力替代方案，兼具理论优雅性和实用可扩展性。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for generative modeling based on a discrete-time stochastic control formulation of measure transport. Adapting classic results from control theory, we formulate our problem as a linear program whose dual variables correspond to the \emph{optimal value function} of the control problem, which directly encodes the optimal control policy. Exploiting this LP formulation, we develop an efficient simulation-free primal-dual algorithm for computing approximately optimal value functions and the associated \emph{value-driven transport} (VDT) policies which approximate the true optimal policy. We show that well-trained VDT policies enjoy numerous favorable properties in comparison with other state-of-the-art methods based on flows, diffusions, or Schrödinger bridges: they lead to straight transport paths which can be simulated quickly and robustly, and can be enhanced in all the same ways as diffusion and flow-based models (e.g., conditional generation, classifier-free guidance, unpaired data-to-data translation are all easy to incorporate). We evaluate our methodology in a range of experiments, with results that indicate strong performance and good potential for scalability.

</details>


### [219] [Disentanglement Beyond Generative Models with Riemannian ICA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22531)
*Edmond Cunningham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出黎曼ICA (RICA)框架，用局部几何结构替代ICA的全局生成模型，通过解缠张量实现无需全局生成假设的点式解缠，在源恢复任务中优于传统ICA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解缠理论（如ICA及其非线性变体）依赖统计独立的潜变量生成模型，假设过强，难以应用于现代预训练编码器学习的特征。亟需一种不依赖全局生成假设的理论来解释这些特征作为独立变异因子的性质。

Method: 借鉴ICA中径向曲线映射到潜空间坐标轴的思想，利用黎曼几何形式化该视角。核心贡献是"解缠张量"（disentanglement tensor），它编码了二阶的点式解缠概念，依赖于数据对数似然的Hessian矩阵和模型诱导的Ricci曲率。

Result: 在受控源恢复实验中，RICA在多种流形上成功恢复源信号，而ICA基线的表现取决于观测数据的坐标表示。

Conclusion: RICA提供了不依赖全局生成模型的局部解缠理论基础，弥合了解缠理论与现代表示学习实践之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: There is a gap between the theoretical foundations of disentanglement and the practice of modern representation learning. Existing theoretical frameworks, particularly Independent Component Analysis (ICA) and its nonlinear variants, assume a generative model with statistically independent latent variables underlying the data so that disentanglement amounts to identifying the latents that could have generated the data. This generative framework is interpretable and theoretically justified, but its strong assumptions make it difficult to apply to modern representation learning. Modern pretrained encoders often learn features that exhibit disentangled properties without making generative assumptions, yet there is no general theory for interpreting these features as independent factors of variation. We take a step toward such a theory by introducing Riemannian ICA (RICA), which replaces ICA's global generative model with local geometric structure. RICA is founded on the observation that in ICA, the factors of variation underlying a data point can be understood through radial curves emanating from the point that map to axis-aligned lines in the latent space. We formalize this perspective using Riemannian geometry and introduce our theory in a way that is consistent with the existing generative approach. Our main contribution is the disentanglement tensor, which encodes a second-order notion of disentanglement that we call pointwise disentanglement. This tensor depends on the Hessian of the data log likelihood as well as the Ricci curvature induced by the model. In a controlled source recovery setting with known ground-truth sources, RICA recovers sources across several manifolds, while the success of ICA baselines depends on the coordinates used to represent the observations. Our work provides a theoretical basis for studying local disentanglement without assuming a global generative model.

</details>


### [220] [Relational Linear Properties in Language Models: An Empirical Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22532)
*Giovanni Valer,Luigi Gresele,Marco Bronzini,Emanuele Marconato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于KL散度的探测方法，检验语言模型表示中关系线性假设（即固定关系的对象表示可通过线性映射从主体表示预测）的有效性，发现该性质在不同模型、层和关系表述方式上存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型表示中普遍存在线性性质，但实验验证具有挑战性。本文旨在通过更高效的探测方法，严格检验关系线性假设的成立条件及其变化规律。

Method: 基于KL散度设计探测方法，替代了先前的雅可比近似方法，在四个数据集上跨层和跨关系表述方式测试关系线性性质。

Result: 关系线性性质在不同模型上表现不同；存在与已有观察一致的层间模式；关系表述方式的变化对该性质有显著影响。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种更高效的关系线性测试方法，揭示了该性质受模型、层次和表述方式的多重影响，为理解语言模型表示中的线性结构提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Linear properties are ubiquitous in the representations of language models; however, testing them experimentally remains a challenging task. This work focuses on relational linearity: the hypothesis that, for a fixed relation (e.g., "plays"), the unembedding of an object (e.g., "trumpet") can be predicted from the embedding of its subject (e.g.,"Miles Davis") by a linear map. We present an experimental method to test the formulation of relational linearity by Marconato et al. (2025). Specifically, we introduce a probing method, based on Kullback-Leibler divergence, to evaluate this property and examine its variation across layers and paraphrased relational queries. It is also more efficient than previous work; for example, it avoids the crude Jacobian approximations used in Linear Relational Embeddings by Hernandez et al. (2024). Our findings across four datasets show that relational linearity varies across models, exhibits layer-wise patterns consistent with prior observations about linguistic information in model representations, and is differently affected by changes in how the relation is phrased.

</details>


### [221] [F-TIS: Harnessing Diverse Models in Collaborative GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22537)
*Nikolay Blagoev,Oğuzhan Ersoy,Wendelin Boehmer,Lydia Yiyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO训练中异构模型协作导致的off-policy问题，提出F-TIS（Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling）框架，使得不同模型可在同一RL训练中高效协作，收敛性与on-policy训练相当，并在某些OOD任务上性能提升高达12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO训练依赖同构模型分布式推理以保持on-policy样本，但去中心化系统中各方算力和偏好不同，异构模型协作会产生严重off-policy样本，影响GRPO收敛。需要一种能处理异构模型协作的GRPO训练范式。

Method: 提出Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling (F-TIS)，一种GRPO风格训练范式，通过过滤和截断重要性采样方法，利用off-policy样本改进局部模型学习，支持异构模型在相同RL训练中高效通信协作。

Result: 在多种异构设置下，F-TIS与纯on-policy训练达到相同的最终模型收敛性；在某些分布外任务上，泛化性能优于on-policy训练，模型性能提升高达12%。

Conclusion: F-TIS有效解决了异构模型在GRPO训练中协作的off-policy问题，不仅收敛性不损失，还能在某些场景下带来更好的泛化能力，为去中心化异构模型RL训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO have seen great popularity in LLM post-training. In GRPO, models produce completions to a set of prompts, which are rewarded, and the policy is updated towards the relatively high reward completions. Due to the auto-regressive nature of models, the generation phase of such style of training can be extremely time consuming. As a solution, prior work has sought to distribute the inference step across many nodes, working parallel. These works assume primarily homogeneous models in the training in order to keep samples as close to on-policy as possible. This assumption may be impractical in decentralized systems, where parties with various computes and preferences may wish to collaborate on the same task. Thus, decentralized training requires an approach that can handle heterogeneous models - different models collaborating on the same tasks. However, this leads to highly off-policy samples presented during training, which prior work has identified that off-policy samples can hurt GRPO convergence. To enable heterogeneity, we propose Filtered Truncated Importance Sampling (F-TIS) - a GRPO-style training paradigm that can use off-policy samples to improve local model's learning. Our framework allows various models to collaborate in the same RL training run while being communication efficient. We extensively evaluate F-TIS in various heterogeneous setups and we show that it exhibits identical final model convergence to purely on-sample training. Furthermore, we observe in some setups better generalization on out-of-distribution tasks than on-policy training, increasing model's performance by up to 12\%.

</details>


### [222] [ImplicitTerrainV2: Wavelet-Guided Spatially Adaptive Neural Terrain Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22556)
*Haoan Feng,Xin Xu,Leila De Floriani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ImplicitTerrainV2 提出了一种紧凑、高效的神经地形数据格式，通过频谱控制机制、小波引导的空间自适应、导数感知监督和训练后模型压缩，在50个瑞士地形块上达到66.25 dB PSNR，比先前工作提升5.70 dB，参数减少3.2倍，单GPU训练仅需55秒，压缩存储至1.23 bpp且仅损失0.28 dB PSNR，同时支持离网点查询、闭式导数评估和分辨率无关重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有栅格DEM依赖插值和有限差分算子，而先前的神经隐式表示（INR）在地形建模中缺乏显式频率控制、忽略地形梯度结构，且模型过大、训练成本过高，难以实际部署。

Method: 提出小波复杂度场（WCF）从解析小波系数推导空间自适应频率掩码，将高频容量定位到复杂地形区域；利用同一场引导复杂度感知自适应采样；引入梯度匹配额外监督以增强导数保真度；采用训练后混合精度量化和熵编码实现模型压缩。

Result: 在50个瑞士地形块上，端到端PSNR达66.25 dB，比先前工作提升5.70 dB，参数减少3.2倍，单GPU训练55秒/块；压缩至1.23 bpp仅损失0.28 dB PSNR；率失真性能与多种成熟DEM编解码器相当。

Conclusion: ImplicitTerrainV2 提供了一种紧凑高效的神经地形表示，兼具传统栅格DEM不具备的离网点查询、闭式导数计算和分辨率无关重建能力，有望惠及多种下游GIS应用。

Abstract: Digital elevation models (DEMs) underpin terrain analysis in Geographic Information Systems (GIS), but in their common raster form, they rely on interpolation for off-grid sampling and finite-difference operators for derivative-based analysis. Implicit neural representations (INRs) offer a continuous alternative, but prior terrain INRs lack explicit frequency control, neglect the gradient structure of terrain, and remain too large and costly to train for practical deployment. We present ImplicitTerrainV2, which advances terrain INRs toward a compact, efficient neural terrain data format by combining a spectral control mechanism with wavelet-guided spatial adaptivity, derivative-aware supervision, and post-training model compression. At its core, a wavelet complexity field (WCF) derives spatially-adaptive frequency masks from analytically computed wavelet coefficients, localizing high-frequency capacity to complex terrain regions. The same field guides complexity-aware adaptive sampling that concentrates training in high-complexity regions, while gradient matching applies extra supervision to enforce the smooth manifold structure of terrain DEMs for improved derivative fidelity. Post-training mixed-precision quantization and entropy coding reduce storage to 1.23 bpp with a 0.28 dB PSNR drop. On 50 Swiss terrain tiles, ImplicitTerrainV2 reaches 66.25 dB end-to-end PSNR, improving over the prior work by 5.70 dB while using 3.2x fewer parameters and training in 55 s per tile on a single GPU. Our compressed neural format is competitive with several established DEM codecs in rate-distortion performance, while additionally supporting off-grid point queries, closed-form derivative evaluation, and resolution-independent reconstruction, which may benefit many downstream GIS applications.

</details>


### [223] [CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22774)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CogAdapt框架，通过LeadBridge适配器和ProFine渐进微调策略，将临床ECG基础模型迁移至可穿戴设备的认知负荷评估任务，在CLARE和CL-Drive数据集上分别达到0.626和0.768的macro-F1分数，显著优于从头训练的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时认知负荷评估对自适应人机交互至关重要，但面临标记数据有限和跨主体泛化能力差的挑战。现有ECG基础模型虽在临床数据上预训练了丰富表征，却因传感器配置不匹配和任务差异无法直接用于可穿戴设备。

Method: 提出CogAdapt框架，包含两个核心组件：1) LeadBridge——可学习适配器，将3导联可穿戴信号转换为解剖学一致的12导联表示；2) ProFine——渐进微调策略，逐层解冻编码器层同时防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive两个公开数据集上，采用留一法跨主体交叉验证，CogAdapt显著优于从头训练的基线模型，macro-F1分数分别达到0.626和0.768。

Conclusion: 证明了基础模型适配策略在可穿戴传感器的跨主体认知负荷评估中的有效性，为临床预训练模型向可穿戴场景迁移提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.

</details>


### [224] [MambaGaze: Bidirectional Mamba with Explicit Missing Data Modeling for Cognitive Load Assessment from Eye-Gaze Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22775)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MambaGaze框架，通过XMD编码处理眼动追踪数据中的频繁缺失问题，并利用双向Mamba-2以线性计算复杂度建模长程时间依赖，在认知负荷评估任务上以低功耗实现实时高精度推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现基于眼动追踪信号的实时认知负荷评估，以支持自适应人机交互和安全关键应用（如驾驶员警觉性监控、自动驾驶舱辅助），但面临数据频繁缺失（眨眼、跟踪失败）和长程时间依赖高效建模两大挑战。

Method: 提出MambaGaze框架，包含两个核心组件：1) XMD编码，通过将原始特征与观测掩码和时间增量拼接，显式建模数据不确定性；2) 双向Mamba-2，以线性计算复杂度捕获时间依赖关系。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive数据集上，留一被试评估下分别达到76.8%和73.1%准确率，超越CNN、Transformer、ResNet、VGG基线4-12个百分点；在NVIDIA Jetson平台上实现43-68 FPS实时推理，功耗低于7.5W。

Conclusion: MambaGaze框架有效解决了眼动追踪数据缺失和长程依赖建模两大挑战，在准确性和边缘部署效率上均优于现有方法，具备可穿戴认知负荷监测的实用可行性。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment from eye-tracking signals could potentially enable adaptive human-centered-AI such as safety-critical applications such as driver vigilance monitoring or automated flight deck assistance, yet two challenges persist: handling frequent data missingness from blinks and tracking failures, and efficiently modeling long-range temporal dependencies. We propose MambaGaze, a framework that addresses these challenges through 1) XMD encoding, which augments raw features with observation masks and time-deltas to explicitly model data uncertainty, and 2) bidirectional Mamba-2, which captures temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity. Experiments on CLARE and CL-Drive datasets under leave-one-subject-out evaluation show that MambaGaze achieves 76.8% and 73.1% accuracy, respectively, outperforming CNN, Transformer, ResNet, and VGG baselines by 4-12 percentage points. Edge deployment benchmarks on NVIDIA Jetson platforms demonstrate real-time inference at 43-68 FPS with power consumption below 7.5W, confirming feasibility for wearable cognitive load monitoring.

</details>


### [225] [Regret-Based $(ε,δ)$-optimal Stopping Criteria for Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22561)
*Haowei Wang,Jingyi Wang,Qiyu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对贝叶斯优化中缺乏理论支撑的停止准则问题，提出了基于GP-UCB的更紧瞬时遗憾界，并据此设计了能在高概率下保证ε-最优解的停止准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯优化通常在固定评估预算耗尽后终止，这不仅可能产生不必要的计算成本，而且无法对解的质量提供最优性保证。尽管已有一些经验性的停止准则，但缺乏理论上的严格性。

Method: 首先推导了GP-UCB在任意迭代轮次下更紧的瞬时遗憾界（provably tighter instantaneous regret bounds），然后基于该理论界限设计了停止准则，该准则能在以高概率1-δ保证ε-最优解时终止迭代。

Result: 数值实验验证了所提停止准则的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于理论遗憾界的停止准则为贝叶斯优化提供了有理论保证的终止条件，在保证解质量的同时避免了不必要的计算开销。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a widely used iterative black-box optimization method that utilizes Gaussian process (GP) surrogate models. In practice, BO is typically terminated after a fixed evaluation budget is exhausted, which can incur unnecessary cost and provides no optimality guarantee on solution quality. Recent research in developing a practical stopping criterion has made empirical progress, yet a theoretically sound stopping criterion remains a work in progress. In this work, we present provably tighter instantaneous regret bounds for GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB) at any given iteration. Then, we propose stopping criteria for GP-UCB based on this tighter bound that ensures an $ε$-optimal solution with high probability $1-δ$ upon termination. Numerical experiments are performed to validate and demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our stopping criteria.

</details>


### [226] [SDPM: Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model for Continuous-Time Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22776)
*Stanislav R. Kirpichenko,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Lev V. Utkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生存扩散概率模型(SDPM)，一种基于去噪扩散模型的连续时间生存分析方法，无需参数假设或时间离散化，在十个真实数据集上展示了竞争性的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存分析方法要么对风险函数施加结构性假设，要么对时间轴进行离散化，限制了灵活性并引入近似误差。作者希望开发一种无需参数假设且不依赖时间离散化的连续时间生存分析生成式方法。

Method: SDPM使用去噪扩散模型对生存结果的条件分布ℙ(T,δ|x)进行建模。在条件独立删失假设下，将模型生成的条件样本通过Kaplan-Meier估计器转换为生存函数估计。模型在变换后的目标空间操作，使用标准化对数时间以及删失指示器的连续高斯混合表示。

Result: 在十个真实生存数据集上与五种强基线（基于树、基于提升和神经生存模型）比较，SDPM在C-index、时间依赖AUC和综合Brier评分上达到竞争性表现。在合成Cox-Weibull数据上，当生成足够多样本时，SDPM能比强非参数基线更准确地恢复底层连续生存分布形状。消融研究证实了目标空间变换的重要性。

Conclusion: SDPM是一种有效的生成式连续时间生存分析方法，避免了参数假设和时间离散化，取得了竞争性的预测性能，且代码已公开。

Abstract: Survival analysis aims to estimate a time-to-event distribution from data with censored observations. Many existing methods either impose structural assumptions on the hazard function or discretize the time axis, which may limit flexibility and introduce approximation errors. We propose the Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model (SDPM), a generative approach to continuous-time survival analysis. SDPM models the conditional distribution of the survival outcome, represented by the pair of observed time and censoring indicator, $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$, using a denoising diffusion model. Under the assumption of conditionally independent censoring, conditional samples generated by the model can be transformed into survival function estimates using the Kaplan-Meier estimator. This formulation avoids parametric assumptions on the event-time distribution and does not require a discretization of the output time space. The model operates in a transformed target space, using standardized log-times and a continuous Gaussian-mixture representation of the censoring indicator. We evaluate SDPM on ten real survival datasets and compare it with five strong baselines, including tree-based, boosting-based, and neural survival models. Results show that SDPM achieves competitive predictive performance across C-index, integrated time-dependent AUC, and integrated Brier score. A study on synthetic Cox-Weibull data demonstrates that SDPM can recover the shape of an underlying continuous survival distribution more accurately than a strong nonparametric baseline when sufficiently many samples are generated. An ablation study confirms the importance of the proposed target-space transformations, which improve event-rate calibration, reduce invalid generated times, and provide consistent gains in predictive discrimination. Codes implementing the proposed model are publicly available.

</details>


### [227] [The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22800)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证鲁棒性、域适应、光度与遮挡不变性、组合泛化、时间鲁棒性、对齐安全性和经典各向异性正则化等问题的共享结构是一个统计问题：估计保持标签的部署扰动的协方差，然后沿其范围覆盖该协方差的矩阵正则化编码器雅可比矩阵（匹配原则）。CORAL、对抗训练、IRM、数据增强、度量学习、雅可比惩罚和对齐风格约束都是该对象的不同估计器。在线性高斯模型中给出闭式最优解，并引入轨迹偏差指数（TDI），通过13个预注册实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前鲁棒性、域适应、不变性学习等问题被当作独立问题处理，有各自独立的方法族，但它们共享深层结构。本文旨在统一这些问题的统计本质，并提供一个可证伪的理论框架。

Method: 通过匹配原则将多个看似独立的问题归结为估计部署扰动协方差并正则化编码器雅可比矩阵的统一框架；在线性高斯模型中推导闭式最优解（定理A）、必要条件（定理G）等理论结果；引入轨迹偏差指数（TDI）作为无标签的嵌入敏感性探针；通过13个预注册实验（从经典ML到Qwen2.5-7B）验证理论预测。

Result: 13个预注册实验中12个通过验证，唯一例外（Office-31）是实验前已指出的特征间隙失败。在7B规模上，匹配的style-PMH改进了选择性诚实性并保持了Style TDI，而标准DPO使其退化。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是命名了部署扰动协方差，阐明了正则化器的必要条件，并在识别该对象后提供了闭式可证伪理论，而非追求在每一个排行榜上的通用性。

Abstract: Robustness, domain adaptation, photometric and occlusion invariance, compositional generalisation, temporal robustness, alignment safety, and classical anisotropic regularisation are usually treated as separate problems with separate method families. This paper argues that much of their shared structure is one statistical problem: estimate the covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularise the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range covers that covariance (the matching principle). CORAL, adversarial training, IRM, augmentation, metric learning, Jacobian penalties, and alignment-style constraints are different estimators of that object, not independent robustness tricks.
  In the linear-Gaussian model we prove closed-form optimality (Theorem A), including cube-root water-filling within the matched range; necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties (Theorem G); the same range dichotomy at deep global minima; and two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E), with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7) for estimation under standard identifiability assumptions.
  We introduce the Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a label-free probe of embedding sensitivity when task accuracy or Jacobian Frobenius norm is insufficient.
  Thirteen pre-registered blocks from classical ML through Qwen2.5-7B test the predicted matched, then isotropic, then wrong-W ordering on geometry and deployment drift; twelve pass, and the sole exception (Office-31) is an eigengap failure named before the run. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty and preserves Style TDI where standard DPO degrades it.
  The contribution is naming the deployment nuisance covariance, stating what the regulariser must do, and supplying a closed-form falsifiable theory once that object is identified, not universality on every leaderboard.

</details>


### [228] [Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization for LLM-Guided Program Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22613)
*Halil Alperen Gozeten,Xuechen Zhang,Emrullah Ildiz,Ege Onur Taga,Tara Javidi,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EMO-STA两阶段框架，先跨任务族进化共享程序存档，再适配到各目标任务，在8个任务族上超越单任务进化，并缓解低证据场景下的过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM引导的进化搜索方法对每个任务独立优化，忽略了相关任务间可复用的结构，导致计算效率低下且易过拟合。

Method: 提出EMO-STA（Shared-Then-Adapt）两阶段框架：(1) 共享进化阶段：在任务族上进化可执行程序的共享存档；(2) 适配阶段：探索多种适配策略（热启动、最佳平均共享程序适配、每个目标任务的最佳共享程序适配），将共享候选迁移到各目标任务。

Result: 在连续优化、几何构造、建模和算法优化等8个任务族上，EMO-STA在大多数设置下优于同等计算量的单任务进化；STA Best-Local在分布内适配最强，STA Best-Shared在未见任务上鲁棒迁移；计算分配实验表明将大部分族级预算分配给共享进化始终有益，大致平衡的共享和适配预算通常最优；共享进化在低证据设置（如ARC任务、时间序列特征工程）中通过偏好跨任务泛化的程序来缓解过拟合。

Conclusion: EMO-STA通过多任务进化共享和适配，在计算效率和泛化能力上均优于单任务进化，尤其在数据稀疏场景下能有效缓解过拟合，为LLM引导的程序发现提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent LLM-guided evolutionary search methods have shown that iterative program mutation can discover strong algorithms, but they typically optimize each task independently, even when related tasks share reusable structure. We introduce Evolutionary Multi-Task Optimization (EMO) for LLM-guided program discovery, and propose EMO-STA (Shared-Then-Adapt), a two-stage framework that first evolves a shared archive of executable programs across a task family and then adapts selected shared candidates to each target task. Within EMO-STA, we explore multiple adaptation strategies, including warm-starting from the shared archive, adapting the best average shared program, and adapting the shared program that performs best on each target task. Across eight task families spanning continuous optimization, geometric construction, modeling, and algorithmic optimization, EMO-STA improves over matched-compute single-task evolution in most settings, with STA Best-Local providing the strongest in-distribution adaptation and STA Best-Shared yielding robust transfer to unseen tasks. Compute-allocation experiments show that allocating a substantial fraction of the family-level budget to shared evolution is consistently beneficial, with roughly balanced shared and adaptation budgets often being optimal. Beyond compute efficiency, we show that shared evolution can mitigate overfitting in low-evidence settings (e.g. few training data), including ARC tasks and time-series feature engineering, by favoring programs that generalize across all tasks rather than exploiting task-specific brittle artifacts.

</details>


### [229] [Posterior Collapse as Automatic Spectral Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22691)
*Johannes Hirn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明β-VAE中的后验坍缩实现自动谱剪枝，其中潜在模式根据其对重建的贡献与β设定的阈值关系依次坍缩，并在线性高斯情况下与PCA谱等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示β-VAE中后验坍缩现象的深层机制，将其解释为一种自动的谱剪枝过程，从而理解不同潜在模式的重要性排序和坍缩顺序。

Method: 采用Landau稳定性分析从损失函数推导坍缩行为；定义潜在重标定不变的序参量对活跃潜在模式排序；在线性高斯情形下建立坍缩谱、效用谱与归一化PCA谱的等价关系。

Result: 发现后验坍缩是自动谱剪枝过程；坍缩阈值可用于识别应优先检查的有效变量；线性高斯情况下坍缩遵循均场定律且与PCA分析一致；在WorldClim数据集上验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: β-VAE的后验坍缩不是缺陷而是有益的正则化机制，通过调整β可以自动控制模型的表示容量，实现从最不重要到最重要的潜在模式依次解耦的级联坍缩。

Abstract: We show that posterior collapse in $β$-VAEs implements automatic spectral pruning. A latent mode collapses if its contribution to reconstruction is below the cutoff set by $β$. Equilibrium solutions with different $β$ thus reveal a cascade of collapses as latent modes decouple from least to most useful.
  We derive this as a consequence of the loss via a Landau stability analysis. We define a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active latent modes and whose collapse thresholds identify which effective variables to inspect first.
  In the linear Gaussian case, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, and each collapse follows a mean-field law. We test these predictions on the WorldClim dataset.

</details>


### [230] [Clipping Bottleneck: Stabilizing RLVR via Stochastic Recovery of Near-Boundary Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22703)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Chiyu Ma,Kexin Huang,Haoming Meng,Qihui Zhang,Yuyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Guoyin Wang,Li Yuan,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于裁剪的GRPO风格目标函数中，硬裁剪机制丢弃了边界附近的有效信号，导致RLVR训练不稳定和次优收敛。为此提出近边界随机救援（NSR），通过随机保留略超出裁剪边界的token来恢复丢失信号，作为一种即插即用的改进方法，在7B到30B的密集和MoE架构上显著提升训练稳定性并超越DAPO和GSPO等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR已成为扩展LLM推理能力的关键范式，但其优化过程常遭遇训练不稳定和次优收敛的问题。现有基于硬裁剪的GRPO风格目标函数中，严格裁剪边界可能丢弃了包含有用信号的token，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出近边界随机救援（NSR），一种即插即用的最小修改方法：对略超出裁剪边界的token进行随机保留而非硬性裁剪，通过随机采样机制在期望上实现隐式梯度衰减，但其随机性、边界局部的救援机制被证明优于确定性梯度衰减。

Result: 在7B到30B模型规模、密集和MoE架构上的大量实验验证，NSR作为即插即用方案显著改善训练稳定性，并在DAPO和GSPO等强基线上持续取得性能提升。

Conclusion: 硬裁剪导致的边界信号丢弃是RLVR训练的关键瓶颈，简单的随机扰动即可恢复有效信号。NSR通过随机保留边界附近token的机制，有效解决了这一问题，为RLVR优化提供了一种简洁有效的改进方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for scaling LLM reasoning, yet its optimization often suffers from training instability and suboptimal convergence. Through a systematic dissection of clipping-based GRPO-style objectives, we identify the rigid clipping decision induced by hard clipping as a key practical bottleneck in the studied RLVR setups. Specifically, our analysis suggests that informative signals can lie in the near-boundary region just beyond the clipping threshold, and are therefore discarded by the standard hard-clipping rule. Notably, once this bottleneck is precisely identified, even simple stochastic perturbations at the boundary can recover meaningful performance gains. Building on this finding, we propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal, plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains these slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. While NSR, via stochastic sampling, can be interpreted as inducing an implicit gradient decay in expectation, our ablations reveal that its stochastic, boundary-local rescue mechanism is consistently more effective than deterministic gradient decay. Validated by extensive experiments across model sizes from 7B to 30B and both dense and MoE architectures, as a plug-and-play solution, NSR substantially improves training stability and delivers consistent gains over strong baselines such as DAPO and GSPO.

</details>


### [231] [Reading Task Failure Off the Activations: A Sparse-Feature Audit of GPT-2 Small on Indirect Object Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22719)
*Mahdi Nasermoghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对GPT-2 small在间接宾语识别（IOI）任务上的稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征进行了审计，发现特征17,491（"cryptographic keys"）与任务失败高度相关（d=+2.93），但因果消融实验表明该特征并非充分原因；逻辑回归在原始残差流上即可达到与SAE特征相当的预测性能（AUC≈0.93）。主要贡献是提供了一个廉价、模型无关的审计流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征与模型行为失败之间的因果关系，验证SAE发现的"可解释特征"是否真正具有因果解释力，而非仅仅是相关性。

Method: 使用Bloom (2024)发布的GPT-2 small第8层残差流SAE特征（24,576个特征），在300个IOI提示上比较成功与失败试次中特征激活差异；对显著特征（特征17,491）进行了三项控制检验：因果消融（零化该特征）、表征基线（逻辑回归对比）、种子稳健性检查（5个随机种子）。

Result: 24,576个特征中有146个通过Holm校正显著性阈值，105个达到大效应量（|Cohen's d|>0.8）；特征17,491（d=+2.93）在转移对象为"the keys"时几乎唯一激活，该条件下GPT-2 small失败率93.3%（其他对象仅7.5%）；但因果消融未恢复准确率（6.7%→4.4%）；逻辑回归在原始残差流上AUC=0.929，与top-100 SAE特征（0.927）相当；5个种子中仅1个将特征17,491列为top-|d|特征。

Conclusion: SAE特征17,491是任务失败的相关因素而非充分原因；SAE基增加了可解释性而非预测能力；主要贡献是提供了一个廉价、可复现、模型无关的审计流水线，可在笔记本电脑上运行。

Abstract: We report a small, reproducible audit of which sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features of GPT-2 small fire differently on failed versus successful trials of the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. On 300 prompts, GPT-2 small reaches 79.7% accuracy; 146 of the 24,576 features in the layer-8 residual-stream SAE release of Bloom (2024) clear a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 reach a large effect size (|Cohen's d| > 0.8). The strongest single correlate of failure -- feature 17,491, d=+2.93, Neuronpedia label 'cryptographic keys' -- is essentially silent except when the prompt's transferred object is 'the keys,' on which GPT-2 small fails 93.3% of the time vs. 7.5% on the other seven objects (Fisher exact p = 8.79 x 10^-33). We put this correlate through three controls that a mechanistic claim should pass. (i) A causal ablation: zeroing feature 17,491 in the residual stream across all token positions of the 45 keys prompts does not restore accuracy (6.7% -> 4.4%); the feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause at this layer. (ii) A representation baseline: a logistic regression on the raw 768-dimensional residual stream reaches 5-fold ROC AUC = 0.929, matching the top-100 SAE features (0.927); the SAE basis adds interpretability, not predictive power. (iii) A seed-robustness check: across five random seeds the keys-subset failure rate stays in 75.0--93.3% (the behavioural effect is real), but feature 17,491 is the top-|d| feature in only 1 of 5 runs. The methodological contribution is therefore the audit pipeline (cheap, model-agnostic, surfaces named correlates) rather than any single feature found through it. We release the code, the 300-prompt corpus, the 300x24,576 activation matrix, the ablation and baseline scripts, and the figures. The full pipeline runs on a laptop (Apple M3 Max, no discrete GPU).

</details>


### [232] [Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22724)
*Adrien Weihs,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多任务算子学习，证明多任务神经算子（MNO）架构在近似和统计复杂度上遵循与单任务算子学习相同的标度律，共享表示不会增加整体代价，且与多任务DeepONet相比具有相同的渐近最优率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多任务设置下学习算子集合的近似与统计复杂度，特别是验证共享表示是否会带来额外的代价负担。

Method: 针对Lipschitz多算子映射类，推导近似和统计泛化的近优上界；同时建立参数复杂度的下界并证明相应的极小化最优速率。

Result: 多任务算子学习遵循与单任务算子学习相同的标度律；MNO与基于拼接任务输入的多任务DeepONet在 worst-case 近似复杂度上满足相同的渐近速率。

Conclusion: 共享表示不会增加多任务算子学习的整体代价，MNO架构具有最优的复杂度特性。

Abstract: We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.

</details>


### [233] [Ternary Decision Trees with Locally-Adaptive Uncertainty Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22740)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三元决策树（TDT），通过在分裂节点引入宽度为δ的不确定区域，对落入该区域的实例进行概率混合路由。五种δ估计方法在72个数据集上均显著优于标准CART，其中margin方法在效率和性能上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准决策树使用硬二元阈值划分特征空间，对远离决策边界和恰在边界上的实例赋予相同的置信度，这忽略了边界附近实例的不确定性，可能导致过度自信的错误预测。

Method: 提出三元决策树（TDT），每个分裂节点以最优阈值为中心设置半宽度为δ的不确定区域。落入该区域的实例通过子树的加权混合进行预测，并被标记为边界不确定。提出五种δ估计方法：quality-plateau、class-overlap、gain-ratio、node-bootstrap和margin，所有方法均利用CART分裂搜索过程中已有的局部统计量计算。

Result: 在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上，五种方法的概率路由均显著优于标准CART的确定准确率（Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p<0.001）。margin方法在42/72数据集上获胜，每单位边界不确定标记率的准确率增益为0.104，且无需额外超参数。

Conclusion: 三元决策树能有效识别并处理边界不确定实例，在提升预测准确率的同时提供不确定性信号，对高可靠性要求场景（如医疗和金融）具有实用价值。

Abstract: Decision trees partition the feature space using hard binary thresholds, assigning identical confidence to instances far from a decision boundary and to those directly on it. We introduce ternary decision trees, which augment each split node with an uncertainty zone of half-width delta centered on the optimal threshold. Instances in this zone receive predictions formed by weighted blending of both child subtrees and are flagged as boundary-uncertain, signaling that downstream applications may treat these predictions differently. Crucially, delta is computed locally at each node from statistics already available during standard CART split finding, requiring no external noise specification. We propose and evaluate five delta-estimation methods: quality-plateau (plateau width of the split criterion curve), class-overlap (empirical class-distribution overlap), gain-ratio (split quality relative to split entropy), node-bootstrap (threshold variance under node-level resampling), and margin (SVM-inspired distance to the nearest cross-class training example). Evaluated across 72 OpenML-CC18 datasets with 5-fold cross-validation, all five methods with probabilistic routing significantly outperform standard CART on decided accuracy (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.001). The margin method achieves the best efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of boundary-uncertain flagging rate), wins on 42 of 72 datasets, and requires zero additional hyperparameters. Analysis on three Breiman synthetic benchmarks reveals that margin is self-calibrating on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best track theoretical irreducible error. Experiments on four medical and financial datasets demonstrate practical value: on mammography, node-bootstrap achieves +0.71% decided accuracy by flagging 10.8% of screening cases as boundary-uncertain.

</details>


### [234] [SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22743)
*Javad Parsa,Enis Simsar,Amir Joudaki,Thomas Hofmann,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SeqLoRA，一种基于序列正则化和双层优化的约束持续学习框架，用于文本到图像扩散模型中多概念组合的参数高效微调，在避免昂贵后验融合的同时提升了概念保真度和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法在组合多个自定义概念时面临表示干扰问题；模块化方法要么依赖昂贵的后验融合，要么冻结适应子空间，限制了表达能力和概念保真度。

Method: 提出SeqLoRA，采用约束持续学习框架，通过双层优化联合优化两个LoRA因子；理论方面建立了强收敛保证，将残差层激活建模为矩阵子高斯过程以推导灾难性遗忘的高概率界，并证明从数据中学习LoRA基比冻结基方法更有效地最小化残差干扰能量。

Result: 在多概念图像生成实验中，SeqLoRA在多达101个概念上提升了身份保持和可扩展性，避免了昂贵的融合，并减少了组合生成中的属性干扰。

Conclusion: SeqLoRA有效解决了概念组合中表示干扰与表达能力的权衡问题，为参数高效微调的多概念融合提供了一种理论上有保证、实践上可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.

</details>


### [235] [Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22746)
*Berk Hayta,Hannah Laus,Simon Mittermaier,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对证据深度学习（EDL）中Dirichlet期望目标计算复杂的问题，提出用Dirichlet均值的代入损失近似原始目标，证明了在温和假设下近似误差随证据量增大而衰减，并在语音命令数据集上验证了该简化方法在预测精度和选择性预测方面与经典EDL相当，首次在语音识别任务中通过EDL获得了覆盖度-准确度的权衡曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDL通过Dirichlet分布建模类别概率提供单次不确定性估计，但其Dirichlet期望目标比标准监督学习损失更复杂，导致分析和实现困难，需要简化计算框架。

Method: 将EDL的一阶经验风险最小化目标近似为Dirichlet均值处的代入损失，证明在多种损失函数（如均方误差、交叉熵）下近似误差随证据量增大而衰减；该框架包含标准softmax分类器作为特例。

Result: 在Google Speech Commands数据集上，简化目标在预测精度和选择性预测性能上与经典EDL相当，且首次在语音识别任务中通过EDL获得了覆盖度-准确度权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 所提出的简化目标在保持EDL不确定性估计能力的同时，更易于使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程实现，降低了EDL的应用门槛。

Abstract: Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.

</details>


### [236] [Lumberjack: Better Differentially Private Random Forests through Heavy Hitter Detection in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22756)
*Christian Janos Lebeda,David Erb,Tudor Cebere,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lumberjack算法，通过构建大型随机决策树并应用隐私保护剪枝，结合新颖的层次数据重击者检测算法，在差分隐私随机森林中实现了最先进的隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私随机森林方法在保护敏感表格数据时，性能严重下降至不实用的程度，亟需在不牺牲隐私保障的前提下提高模型效用。

Method: 构建大型随机决策树，然后进行激进的隐私保护剪枝以仅保留足够多节点的子树；核心是提出一种针对层次数据的(ε,δ)-DP重击者检测算法，其误差为O_{ε,δ}(√log h)（h为树高），支持使用更深的树。

Result: 在基准数据集上，Lumberjack持续优于先前的DP随机森林方法，建立了新的最先进水平，特别是在实际隐私预算下显著改善了隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 精心设计的差分隐私随机森林可以弥合大部分效用差距，这是一个有前景且尚未充分探索的研究方向。

Abstract: Random forests are widely used in fields involving sensitive tabular data, but existing approaches to enforcing differential privacy (DP) typically degrade performance to the point of impracticality. In this paper, we introduce Lumberjack, a differentially private random forest algorithm that achieves substantially higher utility by constructing large random decision trees and then applying aggressive, privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes. A key component of our approach is a novel $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data, whose error is $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for trees of height $h$ and may be of independent interest. This favorable scaling enables the use of significantly deeper trees than in prior work, leading to improved expressiveness under privacy constraints. Our empirical evaluation on benchmark datasets shows that Lumberjack consistently outperforms prior DP random forest methods, establishing a new state of the art. In particular, our approach yields substantial improvements in the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets. Our findings suggest that carefully designed DP random forests can close much of the utility gap, highlighting a promising and underexplored direction for future research.

</details>


### [237] [Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22765)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散扩散模型中参数化方式与训练目标之间的不匹配问题，揭示了统一扩散模型（UDM）中标准桥接参数化与去噪后验之间的差异，提出了leave-one-out后验、精确转换公式、改进的推理采样方法以及吸收态重构，实验证明参数化和采样设计比边际分布选择本身更影响模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型中，clean-data预测可用于不同方式定义反向动力学，但现有工作在UDM中参数化与训练目标存在不匹配：标准桥接参数化并非由去噪后验优化，而是由leave-one-out后验优化，这导致plug-in ELBO与通常的交叉熵去噪目标不一致。作者旨在厘清这一关系并提出改进方案。

Method: 1) 识别并形式化UDM中参数化与训练目标的不匹配问题，提出leave-one-out后验；2) 推导去噪器、leave-one-out后验和分数之间的精确转换公式；3) 提出无需额外训练的推理改进：基于informed predictor-corrector采样器和改进的温度采样；4) 提出吸收态重构（absorbing-state reformulation），保留UDM联合分布但分解为类似掩码扩散的采样操作，具有更简单的去噪后验、carry-over unmasking和自然remasking机制。

Result: 在语言建模任务上，leave-one-out参数化持续改进UDM生成质量，吸收态构造方法匹配或超越掩码扩散（MDM）的性能。这表明掩码扩散与均匀扩散之间的经验差距主要由参数化和采样设计驱动，而非边际分布本身的差异。

Conclusion: 参数化和采样设计是影响离散扩散模型性能的关键因素，通过纠正参数化与训练目标的不匹配，UDM可以取得与MDM相当甚至更优的结果。代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor.
  We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.

</details>


### [238] [Integrable Elasticity via Neural Demand Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22820)
*Carlos Heredia,Daniel Roncel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ICDN，一种需求优先的神经模型，将 log-需求建模为 log-价格的平滑条件函数，可精确推导弹性。在 Dominick's 啤酒数据集上，ICDN 相比基线模型有更好的泛化性能和更稳定、经济合理的弹性估计，尤其是弱识别交叉价格效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零售需求模型在估计价格弹性（尤其是交叉价格弹性）时存在不稳定、经济上不合理的问题，且传统对数-对数基准模型在弱识别场景下泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出 ICDN 模型，采用需求优先的神经架构，将 log-需求学习为 log-价格的平滑、上下文条件函数，从而能够从学习的需求曲面上精确推导价格弹性。

Result: 在 Dominick's 啤酒数据集上，ICDN 在样本外泛化上优于有向对数-对数基线模型，并且产生了更稳定、经济上更合理的弹性估计，尤其是在弱识别的交叉价格效应方面。

Conclusion: ICDN 能够有效提升多产品零售需求建模中弹性估计的稳定性和经济合理性，特别适用于交叉价格效应难以识别的场景。

Abstract: We propose the Integrable Context-Dependent Demand Network (ICDN), a demand-first neural model for multiproduct retail demand. The model learns log-demand as a smooth, context-conditioned function of log-prices, allowing elasticities to be derived exactly from the learned demand surface. On the Dominick's beer dataset, ICDN improves out-of-sample generalization over a directed log-log benchmark and yields more stable, economically plausible elasticity estimates, especially for weakly identified cross-price effects.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [On Moment-Based Recovery of Measures with Atomic and Continuous Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21644)
*Ruben Karapetyan,Shenyuan Ma,Aleš Wodecki,Jakub Mareček*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的从矩恢复概率测度的问题框架，该框架不要求测度具有有限原子性，而是假设测度具有紧支撑和温和的分离准则。通过研究GNS构造的谱表示与正交多项式的联系，作者证明了三种不同情境下的恢复保证，并提出了新的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的矩恢复问题依赖平展扩展性质，但该性质仅适用于有限原子测度，许多测度类别不满足该条件。作者旨在突破这一限制，提出一个能同时覆盖有限原子测度和连续分量测度的恢复问题新框架。

Method: 研究Gelfand-Naimark-Segal (GNS) 构造的谱表示及其与正交多项式的联系，利用该理论工具分析三种不同情境下的恢复保证，并基于理论推导设计新算法。

Result: 提出了一个覆盖有限原子和连续分量测度的新恢复问题框架；证明了三种情境下不同的恢复保证；给出了关于正交多项式与矩恢复算法联系的若干新见解；提出了新算法并通过基准测试验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将矩恢复问题从有限原子测度推广到更广泛的测度类（包含连续分量），为矩-SOS层级方法在更一般场景下的应用提供了理论基础，并给出了可实践的算法方案。

Abstract: Recovering probability measures from moments is a central theme in statistics and optimization. In particular, we focus on the recovery of measures from moments and pseudo-moments, which may come from solving the moment-SOS hierarchy in one dimension. A typical strategy when recovering a measure from moments is to verify the flat-extension property, which certifies that the underlying measure is finitely atomic and ultimately leads to recovery. For many classes of measures, however, the flat extension never occurs and thus if one aims to recover the measure corresponding to the moments, assumptions need to be made. We formulate a new kind of recovery problem, where one assumes that the measure has compact support and a fulfills a mild separation criterion. The key feature of this recovery problem formulation is that it covers not only finitely atomic measures, but also measures with continuous components. We study this new problem and describe three situations in which different guarantees can be proven. These guarantees are developed by studying the spectral representation of the Gelfand-Naimark-Segal construction and its connection to orthogonal polynomials, which ultimately allows us to provide several additional insights, which apply to algorithms widely used for the recovery of atomic measures from moments. Furthermore, the statements proven lead to novel algorithms, which we benchmark, further confirming the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [240] [Proximal Gradient-based Low Rank Tensor Decomposition for State Dependent Riccati Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21885)
*Jiahua Jiang,Carmeliza Navasca*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CP张量分解的降阶方法，用于求解大规模离散控制系统的最优控制问题，通过稀疏优化和混合方法获得低秩CP张量基元素，进而高效求解降阶的状态依赖Riccati方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带有大规模离散维控制系统的偏微分方程最优控制问题，现有方法计算成本过高，需要有效的降阶模型来降低计算复杂度。

Method: 利用快照数据构建张量数据集，通过CP（Canonical Polyadic）分解寻求基元素；采用稀疏优化和灵活混合方法进行降维，获得低秩CP张量基；基于降阶模型构建状态依赖Riccati方程并高效求解。

Result: 降阶后的最优控制问题转化为降阶的状态依赖Riccati方程，可被高效求解。

Conclusion: CP张量分解结合稀疏优化与混合方法能有效降低大规模控制系统的维度，使原本计算复杂的最优控制问题变得可解且高效。

Abstract: We address the optimal control problems arising from partial differential equations with large discrete dimensional control systems. To obtain reduced order models, we find basis elements from the canonical polyadic (CP) decomposition. Tensor datasets are from snapshots of the large models. Our method to reduce the control system is to use dimensionality reduction approaches through sparse optimization and flexible hybrid methods is to obtain low rank CP tensor basis elements. The reduced optimal control problem leads to reduced state-dependent Riccati Equations which can be solved efficiently.

</details>


### [241] [Exact Uniform L1 Spacing for Solow-Polasky Diversity on Lines and Ordered Pareto Fronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21922)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich,Mahboubeh Nezhadmoghaddam,Jesús Guillermo Falcón Cardona*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在指数核下，最大化逆矩阵Solow–Polasky多样性的固定基数子集选择问题。主要定理证明，在[0,1]区间上，唯一最优的k点子集是等间距集；该结论通过等距性推广到有序L1曲线，进而应用于单调双目标Pareto前沿的均匀间距近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在固定基数条件下，最大化逆矩阵Solow–Polasky多样性（等价于指数核的有限度量幅值）的最优子集结构，并揭示该目标函数与均匀间距表示之间的内在联系，最终应用于多目标优化中Pareto前沿的近似表示。

Method: 从已知的有限线间隙公式出发（将过剩逆矩阵多样性写为连续间隙函数之和），证明区间定理（唯一最优为等间距集）；通过逆核命题证明相邻间隙可加结构迫使核为指数族；利用等距性将结论从实直线推广到有序L1曲线和单调双目标Pareto前沿。

Result: (1) 对[0,1]上任意k≥2，唯一最大化k点子集为等间距集；(2) 在归一化非增距离核中，要求相邻间隙可加结构唯一确定指数族；(3) 区间定理通过等距性转移到有序L1曲线，最优集在累积L1长度上均匀分布；(4) 单调双目标Pareto前沿的Solow–Polasky最优有限近似在目标空间变化累积上均匀分布。

Conclusion: 逆矩阵Solow–Polasky多样性在指数核下自然地选择均匀间隙表示，该性质通过等距性适用于更广泛的L1度量空间，为Pareto前沿的均匀采样提供了一种理论最优的准则。

Abstract: We study fixed-cardinality maximization of the inverse-matrix Solow--Polasky diversity, equivalently finite metric magnitude for the exponential kernel, on one-dimensional and ordered metric sets. The analysis starts from the known finite-line gap formula for the exponential kernel, which writes the excess inverse-matrix diversity as a sum of functions of consecutive gaps. Building on this formula, the main interval theorem proves that, for every $k\geq 2$, the unique maximizing $k$-point subset of $[0,1]$ is the equally spaced set. Thus the objective selects a uniform gap representation on the real line. A converse kernel proposition shows that, among normalized non-increasing distance kernels, requiring the corresponding adjacent-gap additive structure forces the exponential family. Further results transfer the interval theorem to ordered $\ell_1$ (L1, or Manhattan) curves by isometry: the maximizing sets are uniform in accumulated $\ell_1$ length. As a consequence, monotone biobjective Pareto fronts admit Solow--Polasky optimal finite approximations that are uniformly spaced in accumulated objective-space change, a natural representation when all parts of a continuous front should be covered. Examples, including a dense connected front and a finite disconnected ZDT3 front, illustrate how the continuous uniform-gap result appears on discrete candidate sets.
  Solow-Polasky diversity; diversity measures; finite metric magnitude; L1 distance; uniform spacing; Pareto-front approximation; multiobjective optimization; fixed-cardinality subset selection

</details>


### [242] [Achieving Directional-Stationarity from a Single Random Direction Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22045)
*Dan Greenstein,Nadav Hallak*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种随机方向探索步骤，可附加于任何基础优化方法，在非光滑非凸约束优化中保证所有聚点几乎必然达到方向平稳性，同时不破坏原方法的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非光滑非凸约束优化中，标准方法通常只能保证弱平稳性，而实现方向平稳性（d-stationarity）一直较为困难。本文旨在寻找一种简单有效的方法，在温和的正则性条件下获得强最优性保证。

Method: 提出随机方向探索步骤：在任何基础优化方法上附加一次探索步骤，随机采样方向和步长，基于函数值比较接受候选点。该方法独立于底层方法的行为，仅需目标函数满足局部Lipschitz连续和方向导数存在且连续。

Result: 理论证明所有聚点几乎必然达到方向平稳性；在DCA和prox-linear类方案上，该方法能保持原基础方法的收敛速率；数值实验验证了探索步骤的有效性和理论保证。

Conclusion: 随机方向探索是一种通用、简单且有效的策略，能够将弱平稳性提升为方向平稳性，且不牺牲收敛速率，为非光滑非凸优化提供了更强的理论保证。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of obtaining strong optimality guarantees in constrained nonsmooth nonconvex optimization under mild regularity conditions, namely local Lipschitz continuity and existence and continuity of directional derivatives. While standard methods typically ensure weak stationarity notions, achieving directional (d-)stationarity remains nontrivial. We show that a random direction exploration step is sufficient to attain d-stationarity. The proposed approach augments any base optimization method with a single exploration step that samples a direction and step size and accepts the candidate based on a function value comparison. The resulting scheme guarantees that all accumulation points are d-stationary almost surely, independently of the behavior of the underlying method. Moreover, it preserves convergence rates of the base method, as established for DCA and prox-linear-type schemes. The theoretical results are complemented by numerical experiments illustrating the effect and guarantees of the exploration step.

</details>


### [243] [Online Optimization with Unknown Time-Varying Parameters from Noisy Gradient Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22251)
*Shivanshu Tripathi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究成本函数依赖于未知动态的潜在时变参数的在线优化问题，提出结合高斯-马尔可夫估计、工具变量估计和参数预测的三步算法，并给出了期望跟踪误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实在线优化问题中，成本函数的线性项受未知线性随机动态演变的潜在参数驱动，且算法只能获取有限含噪梯度测量，现有方法无法有效处理这种非平稳、参数未知且动态未知的优化场景。

Method: 1）利用高斯-马尔可夫估计器从梯度观测中重建潜在参数；2）采用工具变量估计器辨识参数动态；3）基于辨识结果预测未来参数并计算未来最小化点。

Result: 给出了期望跟踪误差的理论上界，并通过数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制理论驱动的框架能够有效处理潜在参数未知动态下的在线优化问题，实现了对最优解的有效跟踪。

Abstract: We study online optimization problems in which the cost function depends on latent, time-varying parameters that are unmeasurable and governed by unknown dynamics. Specifically, we consider a strongly convex cost function whose linear term evolves according to unknown linear stochastic dynamics, while the algorithm has access only to finite noisy gradient measurements. We propose a solution that uses control theoretic tools to reconstruct the latent parameters from gradient observations using a Gauss-Markov estimator, then identifies the parameter dynamics using an instrumental-variable estimator, and finally forecasts the parameters to compute the future minimizer. We provide a bound on the expected tracking error. We illustrate the effectiveness of our algorithm on a series of numerical examples.

</details>


### [244] [Incentive-Aligned Vehicle-to-Vehicle Energy Trading via Nash-Integrated Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22363)
*Yujin Lin,Yue Yang,Hao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Nash-MADDPG算法，将纳什谈判解与多智能体深度确定性策略梯度相结合，用于激励对齐的V2V能量交易。在30天连续运行评估中，相比双拍卖方法，社会福利提升61.6%，交易量提升62.9%，Jain公平指数提升40.1%，并在6-100智能体规模下验证了可扩展性与定价稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2V能量交易方法要么需要集中优化存在计算限制，要么缺乏公平性保障，难以协调具有多样化充电需求和不确定到达-离开时间表的自利电动汽车智能体。

Method: 将纳什谈判解（Nash Bargaining Solution）集成到多智能体深度确定性策略梯度（MADDPG）中，形成Nash-MADDPG算法。纳什谈判确定高效的双边定价，而纳什引导的价格接近奖励使智能体学习朝向谈判最优策略收敛。

Result: 在30天连续运行中，相比双拍卖方法，社会福利提升61.6%，交易量提升62.9%，Jain公平指数提升40.1%。在6-100智能体规模、30天连续车辆周转的测试中验证了可扩展性和接近纳什谈判基准的经验定价稳定性。

Conclusion: Nash-MADDPG通过纳什谈判解引导多智能体强化学习，实现了公平高效的去中心化V2V能量交易，在福利、交易量和公平性指标上显著优于基线方法，且具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) energy trading enables decentralized peer-to-peer energy exchange among electric vehicles (EVs), reducing grid dependency while monetizing surplus capacity. However, coordinating self-interested EV agents with diverse charging needs and uncertain arrival-departure schedules remains challenging. Existing approaches either require centralized optimization with computational limitations or lack fairness guarantees. This paper integrates Nash Bargaining Solution into Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient, namely Nash-MADDPG, for incentive-aligned V2V energy trading. Nash bargaining determines efficient bilateral pricing, while Nash-guided price proximity rewards align agent learning toward bargaining-optimal strategies. Evaluation over 30-day continuous operation demonstrates an improvement of 61.6% in social welfare and 62.9% improvement in trading volume over Double Auction, while achieving superior fairness, such as 40.1% improvement in Jain's index. Testing across 6-100 agents over a 30-day horizon with continuous vehicle turnover confirms scalability across population size and empirically stable pricing near the Nash Bargaining benchmark.

</details>


### [245] [Global Convergence of Control-Based Lagrangian Flows for Non-Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22486)
*Simone Pirrera,Francesco Ripa,Daniele Astolfi,Sophie M. Fosson,Vito Cerone,Diego Regruto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于控制论拉格朗日方法的等式约束优化连续时间动力学，针对比例积分和反馈线性化控制器诱导的动力学，利用约束流形几何结构，证明在非凸问题中满足特定凸性性质时的全局指数收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有收敛性结果依赖目标函数的强凸性或边界假设，本文旨在利用约束诱导的几何结构，在更宽松的条件下建立收敛性保证。

Method: 基于控制论拉格朗日方法，研究比例积分控制器和反馈线性化控制器诱导的连续时间动力学，利用约束流形的几何结构进行分析。

Result: 对于在约束流形上满足特定凸性性质的非凸问题，证明了全局指数收敛性。

Conclusion: 通过利用约束的几何结构，可以在不依赖强凸性或边界假设的情况下，为非凸等式约束优化问题建立更强大的收敛性保证。

Abstract: This paper studies the flows of continuous-time dynamics for equality-constrained optimization based on control-theoretic Lagrangian methods. In particular, we consider dynamics induced by proportional-integral and feedback linearization controllers, which have been recently proposed as alternatives to primal-dual gradient methods. Unlike existing convergence results, which rely on strong convexity of the objective function or boundedness assumptions, we exploit the geometric structure induced by the constraints. Specifically, we show global exponential convergence for non-convex problems that satisfy a suitable convexity property when restricted to the constraint manifold.

</details>


### [246] [A conditional-gradient-based single-loop augmented Lagrangian method for inequality constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22539)
*Xiaozhou Wang,Ting Kei Pong,Zev Woodstock*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带多个光滑凸不等式约束的复合凸优化问题，提出一种将增广拉格朗日方法与条件梯度法相结合的算法，并证明了其收敛率与已知最优复杂度匹配，且在一致凸集指示函数情形下实现了加速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理带多个光滑凸不等式约束的复合优化问题时，传统增广拉格朗日方法需要解决子问题，而条件梯度法适用于具有高效线性最小化预言机的场景，将二者结合有望在保持计算效率的同时保证收敛性能。

Method: 在每次迭代中，先对增广拉格朗日函数执行一步条件梯度法（采用开环步长或短步长两种步长规则），再以递减对偶步长更新对偶变量，从而形成高效的单步子问题求解框架。

Result: 在两种标准步长规则下，算法收敛率与已知最优复杂度相匹配；当h为一致凸集指示函数时，进一步获得了加速收敛率。

Conclusion: 该工作为带多个光滑凸不等式约束的复合优化问题提供了一种计算高效且理论上最优的增广拉格朗日-条件梯度混合框架。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimizing the sum of a Lipschitz differentiable convex function $f$ and a proper closed convex function $h$ that admits efficient linear minimization oracles, subject to multiple smooth convex inequality constraints. We adapt the classical augmented Lagrangian (AL) method for these problems: in each iteration, our algorithm consists of one step of the conditional gradient (CG) method applied to the AL function, followed by an update of the dual variable as in classical AL methods with a diminishing dual stepsize. We study the convergence rate of our algorithm under two standard stepsize rules for the CG method, namely, an open-loop stepsize and the short stepsize, and obtain a convergence rate that matches the best-known complexity for this class of problems. We also establish accelerated rates when $h$ is the indicator function of a uniformly convex set.

</details>


### [247] [Output regulation via input-output data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22562)
*Andrea Bisoffi,Wenjie Liu,Zhongjie Hu,Claudio De Persis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅利用含噪输入输出数据（未知外生信号干扰）为MIMO离散时间线性系统设计反馈控制器的方法，通过半定规划在辅助系统上求解输出调节问题，并严格证明了控制器从辅助系统到原系统的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统输出调节问题需要已知系统模型，但在许多实际场景中系统模型未知或难以获取，只能获得含噪的输入输出数据。本文旨在仅基于数据（无需系统模型）设计反馈控制器，以渐近消除未知外生信号对输出的影响。

Method: 从受未知外生噪声影响的MIMO离散时间线性系统中收集输入输出数据；引入一个合适的辅助系统；将控制器设计转化为一个半定规划问题在辅助系统上求解；通过严格分析两个系统解之间的关系，证明所设计的控制器可迁移到原系统。

Result: 成功实现了仅基于输入输出数据的MIMO线性系统输出调节问题求解，所设计的反馈控制器能够渐近消除未知外生信号对输出的影响。

Conclusion: 提出了一种纯数据驱动的输出调节方法，适用于MIMO线性系统，无需系统模型知识即可实现对外生干扰的渐近抑制，且通过辅助系统方法保证了控制器设计的可迁移性和严格性。

Abstract: From a multi-input-multi-output (MIMO) discrete-time linear system, we collect input-output data affected by noise in the form of an unknown exosignal and, from these data points (without knowledge of the system model), we design a feedback controller that asymptotically annihilates the effect of that exosignal on the output. This amounts to solving an output regulation problem purely from input-output data, for MIMO linear systems. The design of the controller corresponds to a semidefinite program and is pursued on a suitable auxiliary system. Such design carries over from the auxiliary system to the original one by a rigorous examination of the relation between the solutions of the two systems.

</details>


### [248] [Characterization of Normalizer of Lie Superalgebra and its Application to Control Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22694)
*Aroonima Sahoo,Kishor Chandra Pati,Tofan Kumar Khuntia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了以李超群为位形空间、包含玻色子和费米子变量的动力系统的控制问题，提出了线性控制系统在超对称框架下的可控性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超对称理论中同时包含玻色子和费米子变量的动力系统具有重要作用，但这类系统在控制理论中的研究尚不充分，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用李超子代数的正规化子（normalizer）在光滑向量场李超代数中对左不变向量场进行刻画，从而表征控制系统；在此基础上详细研究线性控制系统，提出可控性判据。

Result: 给出了线性控制系统的可控性判据，并提供了相应的实例验证。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将超对称框架引入控制理论，建立了以李超群为位形空间的控制系统理论，为后续超对称控制系统的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The dynamical systems having both bosonic and fermionic variables play an important role in the theory of supersymmetry. This article addresses the control problems including both bosonic and fermionic variables on Lie supergroup as the configuration space. Here, the control systems are characterized using the normalizer of Lie subsuperalgebra of left-invariant vector fields in the Lie superalgebra of all smooth vector fields of Lie supergroup. Then, the linear control system is studied in detail and its controllability criterion is proposed along with suitable examples.

</details>


### [249] [Optimization over the intersection of manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22736)
*Yan Yang,Bin Gao,Ya-xiang Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究两个流形交集上的优化问题，证明了清洁交集与内在横截性等价，并提出了一种仅在一个流形上使用回缩、沿两个正交方向更新迭代的几何方法，在内在横截性条件下推导了收敛率，并通过稀疏低秩优化等数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个流形交集上的优化问题广泛应用于各领域，但可行域耦合的几何特性阻碍了优化算法的设计，需要解决这种几何复杂性带来的挑战。

Method: 证明清洁交集与内在横截性等价，从而获得交集切空间上的可计算投影；提出仅在一个流形上使用回缩的几何方法，迭代沿两个正交方向更新：一个方向用于渐近逼近另一个流形，另一个方向用于降低目标函数。

Result: 在内在横截性条件下导出了可行性和最优性度量的收敛率，证明了每个聚点都是一阶稳定点；在球面数据拟合、双曲嵌入近似和压缩模计算等数值实验中验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的几何方法有效解决了两个流形交集上的优化问题，具有理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Optimization over the intersection of two manifolds arises in a broad range of applications, but is hindered by the coupled geometry of the feasible region. In this paper, we prove that the regularities -- clean intersection and intrinsic transversality -- are equivalent, which yields a tractable projection onto the tangent space of the intersection. Therefore, we propose a geometric method that employs a retraction on only one manifold and updates the iterate along two orthogonal directions. Specifically, the iterates stay on one manifold, and the two directions are responsible for asymptotically approaching the other manifold and decreasing the objective function, respectively. Under intrinsic transversality, we derive the convergence rate for both the feasibility and optimality measures, and show that every accumulation point is first-order stationary. Numerical experiments on problems stemming from sparse and low-rank optimization, including fitting spherical data, approximating hyperbolic embeddings on real data, and computing compressed modes, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>
